### Page 2

2  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
上週重要市場資訊
 澳洲 8 月就業人數勁增 3.95 萬人大幅優於預期，但勞動參與率上升至 67.1%，
推升失業率至 4.6%，呈現勞動力擴增但市場邊際降溫跡象。澳洲央行總裁布拉
克日前指出，失業率回升至5%有助於抑制頑固通膨。考量物價長期高於目標中
點，市場預期澳洲央行本週二將重啟緊縮，升息 1 碼至 4.60%，創 2011年底以
來新高。
 瑞士央行（SNB）上週四宣布連續第五次將關鍵政策利率維持在0%不變，符合
市場預期。儘管受油價推升使 8 月通膨微升至 0.8%，但仍處於目標區間，央行
預期中期通膨壓力溫和，現行極度寬鬆立場依然適當。決議公布後瑞郎兌美元跌
至 1.2077。相較全球主要央行相繼緊縮，瑞士央行維持超低利率，策略轉為視情
況調節匯市以穩定瑞郎。
 標普全球公布歐元區 9 月綜合 PMI 初值升至 53.1，創三年新高，製造與服務業
同步擴張，新訂單增速達四年多最快，印證經濟無懼美伊衝突引發的能源成本飆

升，展現超預期韌性。受能源價格傳導加速影響，通膨壓力再度升溫，儘管尚未
浮現薪資二輪效應，但強勁景氣數據促使市場大幅提高歐洲央行提前於10月例
會再度升息的押注。
 美國 9 月綜合 PMI 初值勁升至 58.4，創 62 個月新高；製造業與服務業 PMI 亦
逆勢走強至 57與 58.7，凸顯企業擴張強勁，引發市場對通膨持續性的擔憂。市
場焦點轉向 9 月 30日公布的美國 8 月 PCE、個人消費支出與 Q2 GDP終值，同
日日本亦將發布短觀調查與央行意見摘要。若終端消費延續強勁，將牽動美日央
行政策利率預期。

### Page 3

3  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
 美元兌新台幣匯率走勢  
Sep/29/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週台幣匯價高點在 31.655，低點在 31.866，以 31.790作收，較前一周收盤價
升值 2.8分。 
上週台幣先升後貶，周初，台股創新高，外資連續五日買超台股並匯入，推升
台幣最高來到 31.655，不過逢低吸引進口商、政府基金等買盤進場，台幣主要在
31.700 附近震盪；臨近周末，美國 9 月綜合 PMI 由 8 月的 56 走升至 58.4，創下逾
5 年來的新高，加上美伊戰事持續，皆推升市場對後續升息的預期，美元指數升至
101 上方，台幣跳空開低，回貶至 31.800 上方，最低來到 31.866，不過隨後出口商
賣壓出籠，台幣跌勢放緩，最終在央行調節之下收在 31.790。 
＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週台幣區間震盪。就國際市場而言，美國經濟表現仍具韌性，9月綜合
PMI 由 8 月的 56 上升至 58.4，企業活動加速至逾 5 年來最強，數據公布後美國 10
年債券殖利率升破 5%，Fed Watch 顯示市場對於聯準會 10 月升息預期升高，有助
於美元指數維持偏強格局，預計將給台幣帶來壓力；就台灣市場來看，台股在創下
新高後回落，而外資也結束連續五日買超的走勢，轉為賣超，短線上資金匯入不利
台幣表現，然而，台灣8 月外銷訂單達 1,029.6億美元、年增 71.4%，創歷史新高，
AI 需求帶動出口持續強勁，出口商後續拋匯需求預計將提供台幣支撐。綜合以上，
預估本週台幣區間震盪。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：31.600-32.000

### Page 4

4  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌人民幣匯率走勢  
Sep/29/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
上週人民幣震盪走貶。人民幣中間價在上周連續十天走升，並升破6.7500整數
關口，最高來到 6.7468，帶動人民幣偏升，不過由於美伊戰事持續，市場對於聯準
會升息預期提升，美元指數震盪走強，加上週間公布美國9月綜合 PMI由 8 月的 56
走升至 58.4，創下逾 5 年來的新高，美元指數突破 101 整數關卡，令境外人民幣承
壓，USD/CNH回貶至 6.7200上方，終場境外人民幣收在6.7227，週線貶值283點，
境內人民幣則收在 6.7125，週線貶值 141 點。

＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週人民幣高檔震盪。就國際市場來看，美中兩國完成新一輪談判，美方
表示雙方同意將原訂 11 月 10 日到期的貿易休戰延長至明年 1 月 10 日，並持續就
關稅、貿易及供應鏈等議題進行協商，降低短期貿易摩擦升溫的風險，對人民幣形
成一定支撐。不過，美國近期經濟數據仍具韌性，加以聯準會偏鷹立場推升美元，
預計將限制人民幣升值空間；就中國國內市場來看，近期人行持續偏強設定中間價，
帶動人民幣維持偏強走勢，惟美中利差再度擴大，預計將限制人民幣升值的空間；
最後就技術面來看，USD/CNH日線圖中，匯價反彈至 20日均線之上，顯示原先下
行動能暫歇，上方壓力先看向季線 6.7350附近，綜合以上，預估本週人民幣高檔震
盪。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌人民幣：6.6900-6.7400

### Page 5

5  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌日圓匯率走勢  
         Sep/29/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週 USD/JPY先上後下。週初，美國多位聯準會官員鷹派談話，帶動美元指數
持續上行，推升 USD/JPY延續前一週的走勢來到 157上方；週間，受美國 PMI 強
於預期與美債殖利率全面上行帶動，市場重新提高對聯準會後續升息路徑的押注，
帶動 USD/JPY大漲突破 159的關卡；臨近週末，日本首相高市早苗表態日圓被低估
是個問題，使市場提高對政策訊號與潛在干預的敏感度，帶動USD/JPY回吐大部分
漲幅，終場收在 157.26，週線上漲 56點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 USD/JPY區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、日本財務大臣片山皋月上週
五罕見透露，美國總統川普在美日元首峰會上對日圓過度疲弱表達關切，首相高市
早苗亦回應日圓遭低估確屬問題。儘管近期美元指數維持在 101 上方，但在日本政
府可能干預的陰影下，預計將限縮日圓貶值的空間。二、美國9 月綜合 PMI產出指
數創逾五年新高，疊加美伊戰事推升油價突破100美元與柴油大漲，通膨壓力劇增。
美聯儲理事巴爾表態可能需再升息兩次，芝加哥聯儲古爾斯比也示警能源衝擊具持
續性，推升市場押注 10 月升息機率升至六成，預計將支撐 USD/JPY 走勢。三、就
技術面而言，日線圖中，USD/JPY連日上漲一度站回年線但隨即失守，目前依舊承
壓於季線及年線下方，不過各天期均線趨平，且下方尚有月線支撐，短線尚無明確
的方向。綜合以上，預估 USD/JPY本週呈現區間震盪的可能性較高。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌日幣：155.30-159.30 
前週交易區間 
156.56 159.03

### Page 6

6  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌歐元匯率走勢  
             Sep/29/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
  上週 EUR/USD 下跌。週初，歐元延續前一週偏弱的走勢，加上聯準會官員鷹
派發言，導致歐元跌破 1.1450的重要關卡；週間，儘管油價自高檔回落，但市場解
讀為能源成本仍處高位、難以迅速回落，推升對美國實質利率與利差優勢的預期。
加上歐洲在能源依賴與成長動能較弱下，相對於美國更難承受長期高利率環境，歐
元最低來到 1.1358；臨近週末，歐元跌深反彈，終場收在1.1391，週線下跌 91點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 EUR/USD 震盪偏弱，主要原因如下：一、歐洲央行首席經濟學家連
恩示警，第二波能源價格飆升將推升通膨，預計至2027年中期始回落至目標水準。
德國央行月報指出，受中東衝突、低天然氣庫存及明年醫療改革影響，通膨將居高
不下；夏季雖因出口疲弱與乾旱致增長趨緩，但在製造業回暖與財政支出挹注下，
估年底經濟有望重啟復甦，預計將提供歐元一定的支撐。二、美聯儲官員接連釋放
強烈鷹派信號，聖路易聯儲總裁穆薩萊姆直言當前 3.75%-4.00%利率仍偏寬鬆，呼
籲盡早收緊；芝加哥聯儲總裁古爾斯比亦警告，AI投資繁榮引發需求過熱，疊加持
續性供應衝擊，可能迫使美聯儲加速且更積極地升息。目前Fedwatch顯示預期明年
4 月前可能再升息三次，將不利歐元表現。三、技術面而言，在日線圖中，EUR/USD
在各天期均線下方，且接連失守1.15及 1.14整數關卡，空方動能較佔優勢。綜合以
上，預估 EUR/USD本週呈現震盪偏弱的可能性較高。 
＜預估本週＞ 歐元兌美元：1.1300-1.1450 
前週交易區間 
1.1358 1.1495

7  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
                           新台幣利率走勢分析

115.09.21-115.09.24 
銀行隔夜拆款利率 0.804% ~ 0.830%  
30天期短票利率   1.65% ~ 1.69%  
公債－10 年期-A15109     1.95%  
－ 5 年期- A15108 1.84%

一、 台幣貨幣市場部分，本周央行持續每日減發定存單，令市場資金充裕，帶動短
率靠向區間低位。 
週二央行標售 2 年期定存單 250 億元，加權平均得標利率為 1.638%，略低於
市場預期，且較上次標售結果下跌 2.5個基本點。 
週四隔夜拆款利率區間在 0.804%~0.830%， 30 天期短票主要成交在
1.65%~1.69%，利率較上週下跌 1 個基本點。 
二、 本週市場交投冷清，10 年期指標券 A15109 及 5 年期指標券 A15108 均未成
交，週四等殖系統參考報價中價分別為 1.95%、1.84%。 
台股排擠效應、國際債市走勢偏空，加上油價續處高檔，通膨潛在威脅仍存，
12 月升息可能性未能完全排除，市場氣氛續偏觀望，預期台債殖利率將維持
高檔區間盤整 ， 預估5年券區間為 1.84%~1.90%，10年券區間為 1.95%~2.0%。

## Document 068: 2408南亞科 元富

Source PDF: all report pdf/2408南亞科 元富.pdf

### Page 1

本刊載之報告為元富投顧於特定日期之分析，已力求陳述內容之可靠性，純屬研究性質，僅作參考，使用者應明瞭內容之時效性，
審慎考量投資風險，並就投資結果自行負責。報告著作權屬元富投顧所有，禁止任何形式之抄襲、引用或轉載。 
元富證券投資顧問股份有限公司 台北市敦化南路二段 97 號 19 樓 (02)2325-3299  (1 15)金管投顧新字第 016 號 
公司拜訪快報

南亞科(2408) 
建議：維持買進 
結論：2027 年產業供需吃緊不變，長期 AI 營運占比挑
戰五成

一、南亞科為利基型 DRAM 製造廠，2026 年資本支出
上修至 697 億元(YoY+420%) 
南亞科為利基型 DRAM 製造商 ， 最大股東為台塑集團之
南亞塑膠持有 29.28%股權，主要競爭對手包含
Samsung、SK Hynix、Micron 以及華邦電，目前月產能
為 6.5 萬片，20nm 和 1B 為主力生產製程。依產品終端
應用區分，消費性電子和 AI 分別占營收比重為 60%+和
20%+。公司維持 2026 年銷售量年增 15-20%目標，因
5A 新廠需待 2H27 量產且存貨處於低水位 ， 故銷售量年
增率不如去年亮眼。至於在資本支出方面，2026 年資本
支出由 520 億元上修至 697 億元，其中五成將用於生產
設備 。 此外 ， 新廠將於1Q27 開始裝機 ，2H27 開始量產，
2028 年達成產能 30K， 新廠全產能由45K 上修至 50K，
投入資金為 4800 億元。 
二、客戶新 LTA 生效致使 ASP 季增幅超過六成，3Q26 
25.4 元續創新高 
3Q26 營收 1336 億元(+62%QoQ)，受惠於 LTA 長約價
格調漲，ASP 季增超過六十位數百分比，銷售量和匯率
則分別為季減中個位數和季增低個位數 ， 受惠於ASP 大
幅上揚且折舊費用持穩 ， 毛利率85.7 優於前季之 79.5%，
營業利益 1068.9 億元(+76%QoQ)，稅後淨利 876.2 億
元(+75%QoQ)，EPS 25.4 元續創新高。 
三、DRAM 供不應求延續，但 ASP 增幅將收斂，估計
4Q26 EPS 28.13 元 
DRAM 產業供不應求持續 ， 原廠供給持續向AI 傾斜 ， 由
於合約價已大幅調漲，ASP 增幅將收斂，估計 4Q26 營
收 1474.1 億元(+10.3%QoQ)，毛利率改善至 86.6%，
營業利益 1196.5 億元(+12%QoQ)，稅後淨利 970.4 億
元(+11%QoQ)，EPS 28.13 元。  
四、原廠新產能規劃搭配客戶長約需求，且 HBM 投片
占比增加，2027 年產業供不應求已成定局  
在產業需求方面，全球九大 CSP 2027 年 Capex 1.38
兆美元(+50%YoY)，AI 運算將主導 DRAM 需求成長，
帶動 DRAM 需求從 HBM、LPDDR5 擴展至網路 、 儲存，
至於傳統應用則面臨供給不足以及價格調漲，導致產品
降規或生命周期延長，部分低階市場需求則需仰賴回收
DRAM。在產業供給方面，雖供應商整體擴充產能，但
產能規劃將搭配客戶長約需求，有助長期供需穩定，加
計 HBM 於產品投片占比由今年 22%增加至明年之 30%，
致使 DRAM 新增產能主要用於 HBM 生產。故以整體市
況而言，AI 強勁需求將推動記憶體供需結構改變，降低
景氣循環對於供應商影響，產業庫存仍偏低，2027 年產
業供不應求可望延續。 
在 DRAM 報價走勢上 ， 雖消費端壓力未減 ， 但AI Server
增量需求支撐 4Q26 記憶體合約價漲勢 ， 集邦預估4Q26
傳統型 DRAM 價格季增 10-15%低於前季之 13-18%，
就南亞科而言 ， 營收占比達50%+的 LTA 客戶已於 3Q26
大幅調漲價格，故預期 4Q26 價格漲勢將趨緩。 
五、新廠產出效益於 2H27 開始發酵，長期 AI 產能占比
上看五成 
南亞科今年資本支出從 520 億元上修至 697 億元，主因
為生產設備投資增加，5A 新廠維持 1Q27 裝機規劃，
2028 年和 2030 年投片目標分別為 30K 和 50K， 整體新
廠投資規模達 4800 億元 ， 由於5A 新廠量產預計於 2H27，
故 4Q26-2Q27 出貨量約當 3Q26，至於屏東 3D 晶圓封
裝測試廠預期在 2028 年底前導入矽穿孔(TSV)和晶圓堆
疊等先進技術。此外，儘管資本支出積極，目前AI 基礎
設施(包含 Server、網路、儲存以及系統管理)客戶營收
占比超過 20%， 但新產能擴建主要以AI 客戶需求為主，
故經營階層預期 2029-2030 年 AI 營收占比將上看
40-50%。 
六、維持買進理由 
1)預估 2026 年和 2027 年 EPS 分別為 75.63 元和
119.26 元。 
2)考量 2027 年產業供需吃緊不變，長期 AI 營運占比挑
戰五成，維持買進評等，TP 660 元 (2027 PBR x 2.8)。

## Document 092: 261015_daiwa_TSMC

Source PDF: all report pdf/261015_daiwa_TSMC.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-15
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: TSMC continued delivering “beat-n-raise” prints for its 
quarterly results and guidance on 15 October. While we raise our forecasts 
to factor in its better topline and pricing trajectory yet muted margin 
expectation on an incremental basis, we see likely lack of near-term 
passion from investors on limited positive surprises from its results call this 
time. That said, we believe AI demand remains strong and sustainable to 
support our Buy (1) call on the stock, though patience may be necessary 
for investors amid the current jittery environment.

What’s the impact: Results intact, guidance mixed. Despite 3Q26 
topline of USD46.7bn topping guidance (USD44.6-45.8bn) at TSMC, its 
3Q26 EPS came in at TWD29.4, only marginally beating our/Bloomberg 
consensus estimates on lower opex and higher non-operating gains from 
long-term investments more than offsetting lower gross margin associated 
with higher depreciation costs on dilutive N2 ramp (table on p.2). Guidance 
calls for higher topline for 4Q26 with midpoint of the range 6% above our 
previous forecast, thanks to continued AI strength as customers continue to 
increase demand. Yet margins at the operating level looked muted without 
further operating leverage being expected by the street, due to the margin-
dilutive N2 ramp. We raise our 2026-28E forecasts by 3-10% to factor in 
the better topline trajectory, plus our view of better pricing power.

Lack of near-term passion? Apart from the upbeat topline but muted 
margin guidance on an incremental basis, we left the results call with 
limited positive surprises this time which may not drive any passion for 
investors in the near term to catalyse the stock. That said, fundamentals 
remain strong since AI demand continues to mushroom from accelerators 
to CPU and other companion chips, as language models transform from 
generative to agentic for AI assistance. TSMC implied upside risk to its 
long-term AI accelerator revenue CAGR of mid-to-high-50% over 2024-29 
and, as flagged in our Asian Foundry report, we saw upside risk to TSMC’s 
pricing power for 2027, given the still meaningful supply constraint where 
management reiterated its strong conviction in this multi-year AI megatrend 
and active capacity build aimed to narrow the supply/demand gap.

What we recommend: We raise our 12M TP for TSMC to TWD3,200 
(previous: TWD3,000), based on our finetuned ROE-adjusted PBR target of 
7.5x one-year forward BVPS forecast (previous: 7.7x). We reiterate our Buy 
(1) call with key downside risks of “silicon inflation” and geopolitical 
overhangs leading to global inflation and AI capex cut.

How we differ: Our 2028E EPS is 11% below the Bloomberg consensus 
likely on our view calling for a soft-landing in the chip cycle.

15 October 2026

Lack of passion despite strong AI?

➢ Another “beat-n-raise” in quarterly prints but likely … 
➢ … short of catalysts on limited positive surprise this time 
➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) call with a higher 12M TP of TWD3,200

Company profile 
Incorporated in Taiwan in 1987, Taiwan Semiconductor Manufacturing Co. (TSMC) is the world’s largest 
complementary metal oxide semiconductor (CMOS) foundry in revenue terms. TSMC offers foundry 
services such as wafer masking, fabrication, probing, packaging and testing, to a high variety of 
customers including fabless chipmakers and IDMs. Its manufacturing fabs are located in Taiwan, China, 
the US and Singapore.

### Page 5

5

Taiwan Semiconductor Manufacturing (2330 TT): 15 October 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 15 Oct 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 1
TSMC's board of directors (BoD) has 10 members, including 6 independent directors
representing 60% of total directors. The background of TSMC's BoD is diversified, with members
from industries, academia, and law. Also, its board members include representatives from not
only Taiwan but also Europe and the US, bringing their experience in operating international
businesses to the board.
We believe the high percentage of independent directors and a diverse mix of expertise from the
board members strengthen the functionality of its BoD, as evidenced by TSMC's leading position
in the foundry market.
Capital management 1
TSMC pays its dividend on a quarterly basis. In the past 5 years, it has managed its dividend
payout well, as evidenced by its dividend in dollar terms, where it has never lowered its dividend.
As the leader in advanced technology nodes, TSMC has kept expanding its capacity for
advanced nodes; as such, we expect its capital intensity to increase to the level near or above
40% over 2025-26 (vs. its long-term target of mid-30%). However, we believe TSMC will at least
sustain its cash dividend payout in dollar terms over 2025-26E. We see its balance between
investment and dividend as appropriate.
Related party &
transaction 1
TSMC's sales to related parties and purchases from related parties remain immaterial as a
percentage of total revenue and cost of goods sold in 2026. We view these levels as insignificant
and therefore see limited risk to TSMC's earnings, cash flow generation, or corporate
governance.
S Supply chainmanagement 1
As the leader in the global foundry industry, TSMC approaches supply chain management by
focusing on 2 main strategies: sustainability risk management and local supply chain
optimisation. It requires all its suppliers to comply with its "Code with Ethics and Business
Conduct", and to follow regulations on human rights and conflict-free minerals.
In 2019, 100% of its tier-1 suppliers signed the "Supplier Code of Conduct" and self-assessment
questionnaire on sustainable management. Also, TSMC's raw materials purchased are 100%
Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC) conflict-free. It also requires its critical suppliers to
report on the status of sustainability management in their critical upstream supply chains.
Through these measures, we see TSMC reducing its supply chain risk.
E Water & wastewatermanagement 2
TSMC applies internal and external company resources to develop water reclaimation
technology, implements water conservation measures and uses reeclaimed water in the
manufacturing process. For example, on September 19, 2022, TSMC Tainan Science Park
Reclaimed Water Plant started supplying reclaimed water. In 2025, TSMC aims to replace 5% of
water resources with reclaimed water, and continue to cooperate with the government to
complete the 2nd water reclamation plan in Anping, Tainan. In 2030, TSMC targets to replace >
60% of water resources with reclaimed water.
E
Waste & hazardous
materials
management
1
TSMC's waste recycling rate reached 96% in 2022 for 8 years in a row. Also, the percentage of
waste sent to landfills was less than 1% for 13 years in a row in 2022.
TSMC achieved reducing its outsourced unit waste disposal (kg/12" equivalent wafer mask
layer) to 0.99 in 2022. Furthermore, TSMC has set a goal to reduce its outsourced unit waste
disposal per wafer to no more than 0.5 in 2030.
E GHG emissions 1
TSMC has continued its efforts to reduce greenhouse gas (GHG) emissions to drive low-carbon
manufacturing. In 2022, TSMC achieved net zero emissions of direct and indirect (from
purchased energy) GHG Emissions in overseas factories, and 100% use of
carbon-neutral natural gas in Taiwan fabs. It has set a target to achieve Net Zero Emissions by
2050.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

M
Update
Co-Tech Development Corp.  | Asia Pacific
Announces additional
investment in Malaysia
subsidiary
What’s new? Co-Tech’s board today approved an additional NT$1.4bn investment in
its 100%-owned subsidiary, CoTech Development (Penang) Sdn. Bhd., to be financed
with internal funds or bank loans. This follows the company’s September 14
announcement that it would establish a subsidiary in Southeast Asia (note).
Our view: Our latest checks suggest that, due to strong demand from key

customers, the Malaysia plant will primarily produce advanced HVLP copper foil
rather than the RG-series foil originally planned. The new plant is also likely to be
larger than Yunlin Plant III, where Co-Tech plans to add ~600 tons of HVLP3/4
capacity by end-2027. Volume production in Malaysia is unlikely to start before late
2028, so most of the ramp-up should fall in 2029, contributing to that year’s
earnings. On funding, our checks also suggest Co-Tech will finance further

investment in the new plant’s construction and equipment by issuing convertible
bonds.
See also our initiation report: Asia Pacific Technology: AI’s Hidden Backbone (13 Sep
2026)

Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

## Document 141: 5425台半 元富

Source PDF: all report pdf/5425台半 元富.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-18
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

本刊載之報告為元富投顧於特定日期之分析，已力求陳述內容之可靠性，純屬研究性質，僅作參考，使用者應明瞭內容之時效性，
審慎考量投資風險，並就投資結果自行負責。報告著作權屬元富投顧所有，禁止任何形式之抄襲、引用或轉載。 
元富證券投資顧問股份有限公司 台北市敦化南路二段 97 號 19 樓 (02)2325-3299  (1 15)金管投顧新字第 016 號 
公司拜訪快報

台半(5425) 
建議：維持買進 
結論：2027 年產品漲價效益將更全面

一、台半為功率元件製造與封測廠商，車用營收占比最
高 
台半成立於 1979 年並為功率元件製造與封測廠商，晶
圓製造有宜蘭六吋廠和天津四吋廠，後段封測則有天津
和山東兩座廠房，半導體以及條碼印表機(子公司鼎漢
3611 TT, 持股 35.74%)分別占 1H26 營收比重為 38%和
62%，半導體業務主要產品包含 MOSFET、保護元件
TVS 和 ESD、二極體，MOSFET 產品線主要透過和晶
圓代工廠如聯電合作，非 MOSFET 產品線則以自製為
主。若以產品應用而言，車用、工業、3C 和其他分別占
1H26 營收比重為 59%、16%、23%和 2%。因MOSFET
業務拓展有成，營收占比由 2024 年的中個位數提升至
目前 20%，且車用客戶結構由中系擴展至歐美客戶，產
品組合優化為毛利率改善另一主因。 
二、報價逐季調整，2H26 營收獲利可望季季高 
除 7 月調整部分產品價格外，鼎翰併購 Bluebird 綜效顯
現並開始出貨大型企業專案，預估 3Q25 營收 52.35 億
元(+3%QoQ)，受惠於無一次性晶圓報廢影響以及產品
漲價效益，毛利率將由上季之 29.6%提升至 30.8%，營
業利益 5.57 億元(+18%QoQ)，稅後淨利 2.53 億元
(+25%QoQ)，EPS 1.03 元。展望 4Q26，受惠於更多品
項產品將於 11 月漲價，預期 4Q26 營收 53.53 億元
(+2%QoQ)，預估毛利率 31.7%優於前季之 30.8%，營
業利益 6.26 億元(+13%QoQ)，稅後淨利 3 億元
(+19%QoQ)，EPS 1.22 元  
三、去中化轉單效益，以及AI 強勁需求改善產業供需為
營運成長兩大動能 
去年 10 月安世半導體之亂以及今年 4 月揚杰科技被歐
盟列入制裁名單，皆引發全球功率半導體去中化轉單效
應，此兩家業者與台半產品重疊性高，其中安世半導體
轉單效益推估增加單季營收達 3-4 百萬美元，因車用客
戶驗證時程冗長，後續訂單延續性高，至於揚杰科技轉
單效益，經營階層認為尚不顯著。此外，Onsemi 於法
說會上將 AI 資料中心功率半導體市場規模由原先預估
的 120 億美元大幅調升至 500 億美元，雖 AI 營收僅占
公司營收個位數 ， 但AI 外溢需求致使成熟製程產能排擠，
改善功率元件產業供需平衡與價格，且國際 IDM 大廠
將資源轉移至 AI 用高瓦數電源模組與高階 SiC/GaN，
此亦致使車用與工控 MOSFET、二極體供給較過去吃
緊。 
四、產品漲價效益尚未完全反映 
全球功率半導體去中化轉單效應 ， 且今年以來Infineon、
TI、NX、STM 以及士蘭微等多家大廠陸續調漲報價，皆
反映出功率元件市場供需改善，且銅、金價格居高不下
致使成本增加，加計委外晶圓代工和封測成本皆增加，
故致使台半今年 4、7 和 11 月三次調漲報價，但今年產
品漲價效益僅限於產能占比近半之非合約客戶。投顧認
為台半今年毛利率改善幅度來自於稼動率提升勝於產品
漲價因素 ， 然功率元件產業受惠去中化轉單和AI 強勁需
求趨勢不變，加計反應委外晶圓代工和封測上揚成本，
預期 2027 年台半產品漲價效應將擴及部分合約客戶。  
五、維持買進理由 
1)預估 2026 年和 2027 年 EPS 分別為 3.78 元和 5.89
元。 
2)考量目前產品上漲僅限於非合約 ，2027 年產品漲價效
益更加全面，給予買進評等，TP 130 元 (2027 PER x 
22)。

### Page 1

삼성바이오로직스 (207940/KS) 
3Q26 Pre: 파업과 환율 부담에도 컨센 부합 
2026-10-06

제약/바이오. 이선경 / seonkyoung.lee@sks.co.kr / 3773-9089

매수(유지) 
목표주가: 2,000,000 원(유지) 
현재주가: 1,354,000 원 
상승여력: 47.7%

Signal: 파업과 환율 부담에도 컨센 부합 전망  
Key: 5 공장 매출 본격화, 미국 공장 매출 기여에 따른 성장 기대   
Step: 단기 불확실성보다는 중장기적 성장 방향성에 주목

3Q26 Pre: 파업과 환율 부담에도 성장지속, 컨센 부합 전망   
26년 3분기 연결기준 매출액은 1조 3,741억원(+9.3%YoY, +4.0%QoQ), 영업이
익은 6,098억원(-3.7%YoY, +4.0%QoQ, OPM 44.4%)으로 컨센서스 매출액 1
조 3,450억원과 영업이익 6,063억원에 부합하는 실적을 전망한다.  
5월 파업 영향으로 약 20개 배치 생산분(약 1,500억원)에 대한 원가 손실이 인식되
고 3분기 평균 환율이 전분기 대비 약 5% 하락했음에도 불구하고, 3분기부터 매출로 
인식되는 5공장 PPQ 배치 및 미국 공장의 매출 기여로 지속적인 성장이 예상된다. 약 
1,500억원 규모의 생산 차질분은 고객사와 일정 조율을 통해 출하될 예정으로 이에 
따른 4분기 매출 성장을 기대해 볼 수 있다.   
신규 수주 회복과 모달리티 확장에 주목 
시장에서 우려했던 신규 수주는 관세 이슈로 고객사의 컨택이 감소하면서 올해 저조했
으나 4 월 Section 232 결과 발표 이후 논의가 회복 단계에 있어 연내 추가 신규 수주 
가능성은 열려 있다.  
항체 시장의 경쟁심화와 차세대 모달리티 확장에 대응하기 위해 결정한 폴리펩타이드
의 인수는 연내 마무리될 예정이다. 폴리펩타이드의 2028 년 목표 EBITDA 마진은 
25%로 동사 대비 낮은 상황이나, 펩타이드 기반 CDMO 시장 성장에 따른 마진 개선
도 가능하다. 현재 임단협, 환율, 미국 공장 및 폴리펩타이드 인수에 따른 회계 영향 등 
불확실성이 큰 상황이나, 수주 회복과 5 공장 매출 인식 본격화, 미국 공장 및 멀티 모
달리티 확장 등 중장기 성장 방향성에 주목할 필요가 있다.

## Document 182: [207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 외형 성장 지속, 수익성 개선은 시간이 필요삼성바이오로직스(207940) 외형 성장 지속, 수익성 개선은 시_dcb09b7c

Source PDF: all report pdf/[207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 외형 성장 지속, 수익성 개선은 시간이 필요삼성바이오로직스(207940) 외형 성장 지속, 수익성 개선은 시_dcb09b7c.pdf

### Page 1

www.imeritz.com

삼성바이오로직스 (207940)

Company Brief 
제약/바이오 
Analyst 김준영 
junyoung.kim@meritz.co.kr 
RA 김현비 
hyunbee.kim@meritz.co.kr 외형 성장 지속, 수익성 개선은 시간이 필요

3Q26 Review  
3Q26 연결 기준 매출액 1조 4,903억원(YoY +18.5%, QoQ +12.8%), 영업이익 
6,401억원(YoY +1.0%, QoQ +9.2%)으로 컨센서스를 각각 9.8%, 4.7% 상회했다. 
기존 1 ~ 4공장 풀가동 및 미국 Rockville 공장 매출 약 1,000억원과 5공장 PPQ 
물량 인식이 더해지며 외형 성장을 견인했다. 반면 신규 생산시설의 초기 비용 및 제
품 믹스 영향으로 OPM은 42.9%를 기록하며 전년 동기 대비 7.5%p 하락했다. 동
사는 연간 매출 성장률 가이던스를 기존 +15~20%에서 +20%로 상향했다. 당사는 
4분기 Rockville 공장 매출 기여와 기존 공장의 높은 가동률을 감안하여 연간 매출
액 5조 6,135억원(YoY +23.2%)을 예상한다.

신규 생산거점의 외형 확대, 이익 기여는 좀 더 긴 호흡으로 
5공장은 2Q26부터 PPQ 생산을 본격화했으나 상업 생산에 따른 실질적인 이익 기
여는 2027년 하반기에 확대될 전망이다. Rockville 공장은 3Q와 4Q 각각 약 
1,000억원의 매출이 예상되며 2027년 분기별 매출도 유사한 수준을 유지할 것으로 
전망한다. 다만 영업이익 기여까지는 시간이 더 소요될 것으로 예상되어 2027년 이
익 성장은 미국 공장보다 5공장 Ramp-up이 주도할 전망이다.

투자의견 Buy, 적정주가 200만원 유지 
삼성바이오로직스에 대해 투자의견 Buy, 적정주가 200만원을 유지한다. 고객사 수주 
문의가 회복되는 가운데 대형 계약 협의가 진행 중이며 6공장 역시 연말 또는 내년 
초 착공을 목표로 하고 있다. 다만 신규 수주가 실제 계약으로 이어지는 시점과 5공
장의 상업 생산 전환 속도가 중기 실적의 핵심 변수로 판단한다. 추가 대형 수주 확
보 시 5공장 이후의 성장 가시성도 높아질 것으로 예상한다. 
PolyPeptide 인수는 펩타이드 CDMO로 사업을 확장한다는 점에서 긍정적이다. 인
수 완료 이후 다음 분기부터 연결 매출 인식이 가능하나 유의미한 영업이익 기여까지
는 최소 2년 이상 소요될 전망이다. PolyPeptide 인수는 현재 추정치에서 반영하지 
않았으며 향후 인수 완료 시 연결 OPM 희석 가능성을 감안할 필요가 있다. 한편 최
근 법원 항고심에서 배양부터 정제까지의 생산공정이 쟁의행위 기간에도 정상 수행되
어야 할 보전작업으로 인정되면서 파업에 따른 생산 차질 우려는 완화될 것으로 판단
한다. 노사 협상은 여전히 진행 중이나 생산 안정성에 대한 고객사 우려가 낮아질 경
우 신규 수주에도 긍정적으로 작용할 전망이다. 
 Meritz Research  2026. 10. 22

Buy (20거래일 평균종가 대비 상승 여력 기준)

## Document 191: [373220] LG에너지솔루션(373220) Long Trading 구간LG에너지솔루션(373220) Long Trading 구간Long Trading 구간 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[373220] LG에너지솔루션(373220) Long Trading 구간LG에너지솔루션(373220) Long Trading 구간Long Trading 구간 - 기업.pdf

### Page 1

www.imeritz.com

LG에너지솔루션 (373220)

Company Brief 
이차전지/석유화학 
Analyst 노우호 
wooho.rho@meritz.co.kr 
RA 황현식 
hyeonsig.hwang@meritz.co.kr Long Trading 구간  
3Q26P 잠정실적 발표 이후: ESS ↑, BEV ↓  
LG에너지솔루션의 3Q26P 잠정실적 발표 이후 배터리 섹터는 11/3 미국 중간선거
를 전후로 Trading Long 투자의견이 유효하다. 미국 중간선거 이후 특정정당 우세와 
별개로, 미국 Ex-China ESS 공급망 프리미엄은 유효하다. 동사 역시 그간의 ESS 
사업 난제(단위당 수율, 팩 병목)는 점진적 해소될 점이다. 반면 시장의 BEV 반등 기
대감에 동의하지 않는다. EU 프랑스와 독일의 내연기관 퇴출시점 지연 움직임 및 미
국 연비규제 정책 폐지 결정으로 전통 완성차 고객사들향 BEV 배터리 출하 부진은 
여전하겠다. 미국의 BEV 정책 변화시점은 28.11월 대선으로 지연될 전망이다.

ESS 모멘텀: 한국 제3차 ESS 중앙계약시장 사업자 선정 모멘텀 
대다수 시장 참여자들은 2026년 연말까지 LG에너지솔루션의 미국 ESS 60Gwh  규
모의 잔여 수주 달성 가시성에 집중한다. 해당 ESS 사업 수주 가이던스 관건은 시기
보다 방향성이다. 단, 당사가 주목하는 연말 모멘텀은 한국 정부향 제3차 ESS 중앙
계약시장 사업자 선정으로 판단한다. 3차 규모는 1.13Gw로 1&2차 계약 규모(합산 
1.13Gw) 대비 확대되었다. 또한 언론보도상 LG에너지솔루션은 해당 수주를 위한 핵
심 4대 소재 등 전 Value-chain 국산화를 선언한 바 있다. 해당 수주 확정시, 동사
를 비롯한 해당 Value-chain에 긍정적 모멘텀이 예상된다.

투자의견 Buy, 적정주가 51만원 제시   
26년 연중 지정학 Risk, 에너지 대전환 영향 등에 전력 인프라 수요는 꾸준할 전망
이다. 향후 국내 이차전지 산업은 ‘L’ Shaped Cycle 전망, 반면 동사는 스마트카 T사
향 원통형 전지 출하량 증가, ESS 제조업 이상으로 변모하는 과정으로 산업 평균 성
장률 Outperform 가능하다. 투자의견 Buy 유지, 적정주가는 추정치 상향에 51만원
을 제시한다.

Meritz Research  2026. 10. 15

Buy (20거래일 평균종가 대비 상승 여력 기준)

## Document 203: AI交易何时止跌？市场盯着Meta“天量发债”背后的“囚徒困境”

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI交易何时止跌？市场盯着Meta“天量发债”背后的“囚徒困境”.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-25
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

巴克莱认为， AI 巨头争相提前融资形成 “ 囚徒困境 ” ，集体发债将推⾼融资成本，限制信⽤利
差收窄。随着低评级债券⻛险上升、 AI 发⾏⼈信⽤分化加剧，融资供给能否被市场消化，
将成为判断 AI 交易能否企稳的关键。
摘要：
过去⼀周， AI 交易遭遇新⼀轮抛售压⼒，美国股票、债券市场同步承压。纳斯达克出现数⽉来最
差单⽇表现，美国⾼收益债⻛险溢价升⾄ 17 个⽉⾼位， CCC 级垃圾债收益率则攀升⾄ 2020 年以
来最⾼⽔平。股指表⾯波动有限，底层资产的⻛险重估却在加速。
引发市场担忧的焦点之⼀，是 Meta 在公布三季度业绩后释放的巨额融资计划。据巴克莱 10 ⽉ 23
⽇发布的研究报告， Meta 选择提前融资，部分原因是 AI ⽣态内其他企业已陆续启动或计划启动
融资，资本市场的吸纳能⼒正变得越来越有限。
这揭⽰了 AI 巨头⾯临的 “ 囚徒困境 ” ：每家公司都有抢先融资的动机，但如果巨头集体加快发
债，新增供给就可能推⾼融资成本，反过来挤压所有参与者的融资空间。与此同时，对冲基⾦

持续削减杠杆， AI 相关信⽤资产的利差分化也在扩⼤。
AI 交易何时⽌跌，因⽽不只取决于科技巨头的盈利表现，还取决于市场能否消化密集的债券供
给，以及不断上升的融资成本是否会进⼀步冲击 AI 产业链。
股指表⾯平静， AI 仓位遭集中清理
过去⼀周，指数表现掩盖了个股之间的剧烈分化。⾼盛数据显⽰，其 AI 受益股与⻛险股多空组
合较 6 ⽉⾼点下跌约 55% ，年内涨幅悉数回吐；动量策略也出现⼤幅回撤，跌⾄ 2008 年⾦融危机
以来的极端⽔平。
周四，标普 500 指数下跌 1.2% ，仍有 335 只成份股上涨；等权重标普 500 指数上涨 0.7% ，市值加
权的 SPY 却收跌。⾼盛数据显⽰，对冲基⾦在此前⼋个交易⽇中有七天进⾏去杠杆操作，周四
的抛售完全由多头平仓驱动。
这表明，近期调整不仅是对 AI 估值的重新审视，也涉及拥挤仓位的集中出清。但仓位调整能否
带来持续反弹，仍取决于信⽤市场压⼒是否缓解。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

信⽤⻛险向低评级借款⼈集中
据彭博，美国⾼收益债平均⻛险溢价升⾄ 17 个⽉⾼位，⼀级市场出现年内最⼤规模的撤单潮；
CCC 级垃圾债收益率升⾄ 15.85% ，创 2020 年以来新⾼，与 BB 级债券之间的利差也逼近 2016 年
和 2020 年的峰值。
杠杆贷款市场同样承压。价格低于⾯值 60 美分的深度困境贷款规模升⾄ 2020 年 3 ⽉以来最⾼⽔
平。摩根⼤通数据显⽰，过去 12 个⽉，价格在⾯值 80 美分及以下的贷款规模激增近 90% 。
值得注意的是，CCC 级债券利差⼤幅⾛阔之际，整体信⽤市场并未同步恶化，说明⻛险正集中
于偿债能⼒较弱的发⾏⼈。DoubleLine Capital 企业信⽤总监 Robert Cohen 指出，部分 CCC 级
借款⼈可能⾛向债务重组，但这并不意味着整个信⽤周期已经逆转。
⾼盛数据显⽰，从投资级、⾼收益信⽤衍⽣品到低评级现⾦债券，多个评级区间的利差均触及
本轮周期⾼位。 CreditSights 新加坡亚太公司信⽤主管 R. Lakshmanan 认为，多重全球⻛险接连

冲击市场后，信⽤⻛险重新定价正逐渐形成持续趋势。
Meta 发债揭⽰ AI 融资 “ 囚徒困境 ”
Meta 的融资计划将 AI 资本开⽀与信⽤市场供给之间的⽭盾推到了台前。公司将 2026 年资本开⽀
预期收窄⾄ 1350 亿⾄ 1450 亿美元，预计第四季度⽀出达 490 亿⾄ 590 亿美元；分红后的⾃由现⾦
流转负，管理层还预计从今年秋季开始融资数千亿美元。
巴克莱在报告中指出，超⼤规模科技公司的营收增⻓正在兑现，但资本开⽀仍持续攀升，融资
问题已从 “ 能否融到资 ” 转向 “ 何时融、融多少、如何融 ” 。
Meta 在申报⽂件中表⽰，鉴于 AI ⽣态内其他企业已启动或计划启动融资，资本市场的吸纳能⼒
正变得越来越有限，因此提前融资具有现实必要性。
但当所有公司都选择抢先融资，个体的理性决策就可能转化为整体市场的压⼒：新增债券供给
集中涌⼊市场，推⾼发⾏成本，并限制信⽤利差收窄。为避免过度举债损害信⽤评级，企业也
开始考虑股权融资、可转债、资产⽀持证券（ ABS ）及表外融资等渠道。

巴克莱认为， AI 相关融资供给将在短期内继续限制信⽤利差收窄空间。
AI 信⽤分化加剧，市场转向个券定价

### Page 3

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

图⽚
2 条评论
巴克莱估算， AI 相关信⽤资产规模约为 6800 亿美元，涵盖超⼤规模科技公司、半导体、数据中
⼼及硬件企业，占投资级债券指数市值约 7.5% ，却贡献约 13% 的指数久期⻛险敞⼝（ DTS ）。
整体⽽⾔， AI 信⽤资产相较投资级指数的利差溢价约为 35 个基点，期限超过 10 年的债券溢价更
达到约 55 个基点。与此同时， AI 发⾏⼈之间的利差离散度过去⼀年从 24 个基点升⾄ 65 个基点；
即使剔除甲⻣⽂和数据中⼼板块，该指标仍上升约 60% ，达到 37 个基点。
⾃ 9 ⽉中旬以来，甲⻣⽂、 Meta 、 SpaceX 及多家数据中⼼运营商的信⽤利差扩张幅度明显超过
指数；微软、⾕歌和亚⻢逊的短期限债券则相对抗压。市场开始根据发⾏⼈的融资需求、资产

负债表和⻓期⻛险进⾏区分，⽽⾮将 AI 视为统⼀的信⽤主题。
收益率曲线也反映出⻓端压⼒。巴克莱数据显⽰，过去⼀年，超⼤规模科技公司的平均 2 年期⾄
5 年期、 5 年⾄ 10 年期及 10 年⾄ 30 年期收益率曲线分别陡峭化 137% 、 140% 和 68% 。其中， Meta
的 10 年⾄ 30 年期曲线利差达到 68 个基点，在同类发⾏⼈中最为陡峭。
⻓端曲线⾛陡，反映投资者对⻓期债务要求的⻛险补偿上升。随着 AI 企业持续扩⼤资本开⽀，市
场不仅要判断投资能否转化为收⼊，还要评估融资规模、现⾦流改善时间及新增债券对融资成
本的影响。
因此， AI 交易能否⽌跌，关键在于巨额融资能否被市场消化、信⽤利差能否企稳，以及 AI 企业
之间的⻛险分化是否继续扩⼤。在这些压⼒缓解之前，阶段性反弹仍可能受到融资成本上升的
制约。

## Document 212: AI関連企業は巨額の借金を返せるのか ～データセンター投資の急拡大と高まる信用リスク～

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI関連企業は巨額の借金を返せるのか ～データセンター投資の急拡大と高まる信用リスク～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-26
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Research Report

2026 年10 月 26 日

1/6 
AI 関連企業は巨額の借金を返せるのか 
～データセンター投資の急拡大と高まる信用リスク～ 
 資産運用調査部 主席研究員  柏村 祐

（要旨） 
○ 世界的な AI ブームが続くなか、AI 関連企業がデータセンター建設などの巨額投資を賄うた
めに社債発行や借入による資金調達を急増させている。「AI関連企業は手元資金が潤沢で財
務が盤石だ」という従来の常識は揺らぎつつあり、企業の資金不足や信用リスクに目を向け
る必要がある。 
○ AI 投資の拡大は3 つの変化を通じて、企業の財務と金融市場にリスクをもたらしうる。第一
に、テクノロジー企業の社債発行が急増し、借入依存が強まっている。第二に、巨額の設備
投資がAI大手の現金創出力（フリーキャッシュフロー）を圧迫している。第三に、信用力の
低いテクノロジー企業に対する社債市場の警戒が強まり、信用スプレッドが拡大している。 
○ 「AIの成長期待さえあれば巨額の投資もすべて回収できる」と安易に想定すべきではない。
AI 投資の資金を借入などの外部資金に頼る度合いが高まる環境では、収益化の遅れが企業の
信用力低下に直結しやすく、従来の資産配分についても再点検が必要となる。 
○ 個人投資家にとっては、保有する社債ファンドの業種偏りや信用リスクを点検することと、
株式運用において特定の AI 銘柄への過度な集中を避け、安定したキャッシュフローを生む
資産を補完的に組み入れることが、対応策の一つとなるだろう。

1. 「キャッシュリッチなAI企業」の変調 
生成AIの普及を原動力とする世界的なハイテク株高は、日本の個人投資家にとっても資産形成
の大きな追い風となってきた。NISA（少額投資非課税制度）などを通じて米国株式（S&P500な
ど）や全世界株式を保有する投資家の多くは、AI関連企業の成長や株価上昇の恩恵を受けてきた
といえる。もっとも、足元ではAI関連株の値動きが荒くなる場面も増え、巨額の投資が本当に利
益につながるのか不安を感じている投資家も少なくないだろう。一方で、市場には依然として「A
I企業の財務は盤石であり、足元の揺らぎは一時的な調整にすぎない」とみる楽観論も根強い。し
かし、企業の「台所事情」に目を向けると、これまでとは異なるリスクが見えてくる。 「AIブー
ム」と聞くと、華やかな技術革新や株価の上昇ばかりに目が向きがちだが、企業財務の視点に立
つと、巨額のデータセンター投資が企業の手元資金を圧迫し、一部のAI関連企業では借入などの

### Page 2

2/6 
外部資金への依存度が高まりつつある。 
華やかなAI株高の裏側で膨らむ巨額の借金を、AI関連企業は本当に返せるのだろうか。 「テクノ
ロジー企業は潤沢な現金を持ち、財務が健全だ」という従来の常識にとらわれていると、資金不
足や信用リスクの拡大という構造的な変化を見落としかねない。本稿では、AI投資の拡大が引き
起こす 3つの変化として、 「社債発行の急増」 「現金創出力の圧迫」 「信用スプレッドの拡大」を整
理し、個人投資家が資産運用において注意すべき論点を提示したい。なお、本稿では、AI投資を
主導する巨大テクノロジー企業（ハイパースケーラー）から、AI関連事業を手掛ける中堅企業ま
でを幅広く「AI関連企業」と総称する。また、 「テクノロジー企業」は、社債発行統計などにおい
てテクノロジー（IT）部門に分類される企業を指す用語として用いる。

2. AI投資の拡大はなぜ資金不足と信用リスクを招くのか 
(1) AI投資の拡大で急増するテクノロジー企業の社債発行 
現在のAIブームを牽引する巨大テクノロジー企業（ハイパースケーラー）をはじめとするAI関
連企業は、データセンターの建設や先端半導体の確保、電力インフラの整備などに巨額の資金を
投じている。これまでテクノロジー企業は、株式発行や潤沢な手元資金を主な資金源としてきた
が、投資規模の急拡大に伴い、資金調達のあり方が大きく変わりつつある。 
RBA（豪州準備銀行）が2026 年10月に公表した「Financial Stability Review」によれば、AI
関連企業は大規模な投資計画を賄うため、社債やプライベート・クレジット、銀行借入など、負
債による資金調達への依存を強めている。資料1の上段は米国の投資適格社債の発行総額に占め
るテクノロジー部門の割合を、下段はハイパースケーラー（巨大クラウド・AI企業）とテクノロ
ジー部門の実際の社債発行額を示したものである。2026年には、米国の投資適格社債の発行総額
に占めるテクノロジー部門の割合が約27％に達している。また、下段が示すように、足元ではハ
イパースケーラーによる起債が急増し、部門全体の社債発行額を大きく押し上げている。RBAは、
今後計画されているAI関連の設備投資のうち3分の1以上が負債性資金によって賄われるとの市
場の見方を紹介している。つまり、AIブームが続くほど、テクノロジー企業の借入依存が強ま
り、公募・私募の債券市場全体がAI投資の成否に左右されやすくなるという新たな構図が生まれ
ている。

### Page 3

3/6 
資料 1 テクノロジー部門の社債発行が急増、米国投資適格社債の発行総額に占める割合は約 27％に 
（注）米国の非金融企業による投資適格社債の発行額（期間は 2010 年 3 月～2026 年 7 月 17 日）。テクノロジー部門にはコンピ
ュータ・電子機器を含む。上段の割合は 4 四半期移動平均。ハイパースケーラーは Alphabet、Amazon、Meta、Microsoft、
Oracle、NVIDIA を指す。 
（出所）Reserve Bank of Australia, “Financial Stability Review – October 2026”をもとに第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作成

(2) 巨額の設備投資が企業の現金創出力を圧迫 
なぜ、高い収益力を誇る巨大テクノロジー企業までもが借入への依存を強めているのか。その
背景には、データセンター構築などの設備投資があまりにも巨額であり、本業の稼ぐ力（営業利
益）だけでは投資資金を賄いきれなくなっている現実がある。 
IMF（国際通貨基金）が2026 年10月に公表した「Global Financial Stability Report」は、
AI投資の中心を担う主要ハイパースケーラー5社（Alphabet、Amazon、Meta、Microsoft、
Oracle）の財務状況を分析している。資料2は、これら 5社の設備投資（Capex：Capital 
Expenditure） 、営業利益、およびフリーキャッシュフローの推移を示したものである。5社の設備
投資は 2023年頃から急拡大し、2025年後半から2026年にかけて営業利益を上回る規模に達して
いる。その結果、フリーキャッシュフロー（右軸）は急減し、2023～24年のピーク時には四半期
ベースで600 億米ドルを超えていたものが、2026年にはほぼゼロまで落ち込んでいる。もっと
も、ハイパースケーラー各社は現時点では強固なバランスシートと十分な流動性を維持してい
る。しかし、激しい競争圧力や技術の陳腐化に対応するため、企業が投資の手を緩めることは難
しい。今後、AIの収益化が市場の期待を下回るようなことがあれば、企業の現金創出力は一段と
圧迫され、追加の資金調達や財務負担の増大を招く可能性がある。

### Page 4

4/6

資料 2 主要ハイパースケーラー5 社の設備投資が急増し、2026 年のフリーキャッシュフローはほぼゼロに 
（注）主要ハイパースケーラー5 社（Alphabet、Amazon、Meta、Microsoft、Oracle）の設備投資（Capex、左軸）、営業利益（左軸）、
およびフリーキャッシュフロー（右軸）の四半期ごとの推移（単位：10 億米ドル）。 
（出所）IMF, “Global Financial Stability Report, October 2026”をもとに第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作成

(3) 信用力の低いテクノロジー企業に対する社債市場の警戒 
さらに、AI投資への借入依存が高まるなか、社債市場ではテクノロジー企業の信用リスクに対
する警戒が強まりつつある。株式市場ではAIの成長期待が注目されやすいが、元利金の確実な返
済を重視する債券市場では、企業の財務体力やビジネスモデルの持続可能性に対する選別がシビ
アに進んでいる。 
ECB（欧州中央銀行）が2026 年5月に公表した「Financial Stability Review」では、AIを巡
る懸念が株式市場だけでなく社債市場にも波及していると指摘している。資料3 は、2025年 3月
から 2026年5 月にかけてのユーロ圏および米国におけるハイイールド（HY、低格付け）社債の信
用スプレッドの推移を示したものである。米国・ユーロ圏ともに、ハイイールド債市場全体（米
国HY、ユーロ圏HY）のスプレッドが300ベーシスポイント（bp）前後で比較的落ち着いて推移し
ていたのに対し、信用力の低いテクノロジー企業（米国HYテック、ユーロ圏HYテック）のスプレ
ッドは 2026年に入り500bp超へと大きく拡大し、同年 5月時点でも450bp前後と、市場全体を上
回る水準で推移していた。 
ECBは、AIが既存のソフトウェア企業などのビジネスモデルを脅かすリスクに加え、内部資金だ
けでなく借入に頼ってAI投資を拡大させる動きが金融安定上の懸念になりうると指摘している。
巨額のAI投資ブームの裏側では、信用力の低いテクノロジー企業に対する市場の警戒が強まり、
企業の借入依存が信用市場にも新たなリスクをもたらしつつある。 「借りた巨額の資金を本当に返

### Page 5

5/6 
せるのか」という市場の問いかけがすでに始まっているのである。

資料 3 信用力の低いテクノロジー企業の信用スプレッドはハイイールド債市場全体より大きく拡大 
（注）2025 年 3 月 1 日～2026 年 5 月 19 日におけるユーロ圏および米国のハイイールド（HY）社債の信用スプレッド（OIS カーブ 
に対する上乗せ金利、時価総額加重平均、単位：ベーシスポイント）。「HY テック」はハイイールド格付けのテクノロジー企業
を指す。 
縦の破線は左から 2025 年 4 月の米国の関税発表、前回 FSR（金融安定報告書）公表時点（2025 年 11 月）、中東戦争の開 
始（2026 年 3 月初旬）を示す。 
（出所）European Central Bank, “Financial Stability Review, May 2026”をもとに第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作成

3. 「AI借金時代」の資産防衛術 
第 2 章で見てきたように、AI ブームの裏側では「社債発行の急増」 「現金創出力の圧迫」 「信用
スプレッドの拡大」という3 つの変化が同時に進行している。この視点に立てば、AIブームが抱
える死角は「株価の割高感」だけでは説明できない。巨大テクノロジー企業から中堅のテクノロ
ジー企業に至るまで、AI投資の拡大に伴い、一部の企業では自己資金だけでなく、借入などの外
部資金への依存度が高まりつつある。 
ここで重要なのは、 「テクノロジー企業はキャッシュリッチで財務が盤石だ」と安易に考えない
ことである。仮にAI の収益化が遅れれば、膨らんだ借入負担が企業の業績や信用力を直撃するシ
ナリオも視野に入れる必要がある。こうした「AI借金時代」を迎えるなか、個人投資家には、従
来のポートフォリオに潜む信用リスクを改めて点検することが求められる。

### Page 6

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
6/6 
第一に、社債ファンドやハイイールド債（低格付け社債）における「信用リスク」を過小評価
しないことである。2026年の米国の投資適格社債の発行総額に占めるテクノロジー部門の割合が
約27％に達し、信用力の低いテクノロジー企業のスプレッドが拡大している現状では、 「債券だか
ら安心」 「株安を社債がカバーしてくれる」という従来の前提が働きにくい可能性がある。AI の収
益期待が後退する局面では、AI 関連株の下落と信用スプレッドの拡大（社債価格の下落）が同時
に進みかねない点に注意が必要だ。債券を保有する場合には、表面的な利回りの高さだけでハイ
イールド債や社債ファンドを選好するのではなく、組入資産の業種の偏りを確認することや、信
用リスクの低い先進国国債などへの分散を検討することも、選択肢の一つとなる。 
第二に、株式運用において「AI関連だから買う」という一括りの発想から脱却し、企業の現金
創出力と業種分散を重視することである。NISAなどを活用してS&P500や全世界株式連動商品を保
有する投資家は多いが、これらの指数では、米国の巨大テクノロジー企業の構成比率が高まって
おり、特にS&P500では一部の企業への集中が進んでいる。指数連動商品をポートフォリオの中核
に据えつつも、補完的な資産として、安定したフリーキャッシュフローを生み出す高配当バリュ
ー株やディフェンシブ株、あるいはAIデータセンター投資の受け皿となる電力・インフラ関連株
などを組み入れることは、AI 企業の資金不足や淘汰の波に備える選択肢の一つとなるだろう。た
だし、特定のテーマや業種に偏りすぎないよう、投資比率には十分な注意が必要である。 
AI 革命は株式市場に大きな成長機会をもたらす一方で、企業の財務構造や信用市場のリスクを
も変えつつある。これからは、 「AIだから成長する」という発想にとどまらず、その裏側で膨らむ
「借金の行方」まで見据えることが重要になる。AI 企業のフリーキャッシュフローや信用スプレ
ッドの動きも、株価と同様に注視する。それが、巨額のAI投資時代において投資家に求められる
新たな視点であろう。

以 上

## Document 217: Anthropic正式提交IPO申请！股票代码“ANT”，上半年净亏807亿美元，警示人类“生存危机”

Source PDF: all report pdf/Anthropic正式提交IPO申请！股票代码“ANT”，上半年净亏807亿美元，警示人类“生存危机”.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-26
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Anthropic 据报道将于 11 ⽉ 16 ⽇当周进⾏ IPO ，此前部分投资者对其估值从 2 万亿美元下调
⾄ 1.5 万亿美元。公司上半年营收 163 亿美元，净亏损却达 807 亿美元；第三季度， 79% 的
营收来⾃前五⼤客⼾以外的客⼾群体，客⼾结构的分散度进⼀步提升。公司巨额算⼒账单
引关注：与博通、 SpaceX AI 及英伟达、 Nscale 相关⽀出合计约 3263 亿美元。
摘要：
全球 AI ⾏业最受瞩⽬的 IPO 之⼀，正式进⼊公开申报阶段。
根据 10 ⽉ 26 ⽇披露的申报信息， Anthropic 正式向美国证券交易委员会（ SEC ）提交⾸次公开募
股（ IPO ）申请，计划以股票代码 “ANT” 在纳斯达克全球精选市场挂牌交易。
同⽇，据《华尔街⽇报》报道，Anthropic 计划于 11 ⽉ 16 ⽇当周进⾏ IPO 。
此前彭博社报道称，市场传出的⽬标估值⾼达 2 万亿美元，有望超过 SpaceX 此前创纪录的 1.77
万亿美元上市规模。路透社报道，在 Anthropic 于近期⼀场投资者活动披露部分财务细节后，部

分投资者⽬前对其估值约为 1.5 万亿美元，较⼀周前的约 2 万亿美元明显下调。
申报⽂件显⽰，Anthropic 上半年营收 163 亿美元，上半年净亏损 807 亿美元。公司警告投资
者，未来可能⽆法实现或维持盈利。
不过，据路透社此前对申报材料的报道， 807 亿美元的净亏损中，其中约 720 亿美元来⾃可转换
债券及其他融资⼯具公允价值变动产⽣的⾮现⾦会计费⽤，⽽⾮⽇常经营⽀出。
算⼒与基础设施账单规模惊⼈。根据此次 IPO 申报⽂件，公司与博通相关的设备租赁总额约
1612 亿美元，向 SpaceX AI 及英伟达相关安排的⽀出最⾼达 1205 亿美元，另有向 Nscale ⽀付最
⾼约 446 亿美元的服务承诺。三项⾦额合计约 3263 亿美元。
Anthropic 招股书中特别强调，如果其技术在开发过程中缺乏⾜够的安全防护措施和有效监管，
可能会" 对⼈类构成灾难性或⽣存级别的⻛险，尤其是在此类系统被滥⽤、复制、武器化或被赋
予⾼度⾃主运⾏权限的情况下 " 。
此次发⾏由⾼盛、摩根⼠丹利、摩根⼤通、花旗等多家⾦融机构参与承销，巴克莱、富国银

⾏、美银证券、德意志银⾏、瑞银及加拿⼤皇家银⾏等也在承销团之列。
Anthropic 选择在市场动荡背景下推进上市，引发外界对定价时机的关注。⽬前全球投资者对债
务驱动的 AI 投资热潮⽇益审慎，担忧巨额资本投⼊能否迅速产⽣⾜够回报以⽀撑⾼估值。美债收
益率攀升，进⼀步强化了市场的谨慎情绪。
专注于 IPO 研究的机构 Renaissance Capital ⾼级策略师 Matt Kennedy 表⽰： " 尽管 Anthropic 资⾦
雄厚，但 IPO 将为公司带来宝贵的财务灵活性。没有哪家公司愿意在即将上市的临界点上耗费太
⻓时间，因为这可能会分散注意⼒。 " 他补充称： " 也许他们明年能获得更⾼的估值，但他们决
定 ' ⼀⿎作⽓ ' 。 "
算⼒⽀出激增，但成本占⽐持续改善
招股书显⽰，截⾄ 2026 年 6 ⽉ 30 ⽇的六个⽉内，公司在算⼒与基础设施上的⽀出达 89 亿美元，
同⽐增⻓ 283% 。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

然⽽公司特别指出，这⼀⽀出占营收的⽐重已⼤幅下降。
从 2023 年的逾 10 倍营收，降⾄ 2024 年的 653% 、 2025 年的 160% ，再⾄ 2026 年上半年的 55% ，
仅在今年第⼆季度便进⼀步收窄⾄ 48% ，即每实现 1 美元营收，对应的算⼒与基础设施成本约为
0.48 美元。
在⻓期算⼒采购⽅⾯，仅⾃ 2025 年底⾄今，Anthropic 已就算⼒资源签订新协议，最低承付⾦
额约达 4170 亿美元，协议期限最⻓延伸⾄ 20 年。
截⾄ 2026 年 6 ⽉ 30 ⽇，公司总债务为 62 亿美元，并拥有⼀项上限 150 亿美元的循环信贷额度（由
原先的 32.5 亿美元扩容⽽来），该额度将于 2031 年到期。公司表⽰，在获得⾄少两家主要评级机
构的投资级评级后，该循环贷款可转为⽆担保形式。
企业客⼾留存亮眼，分散度进⼀步提升
Anthropic 的商业化进展已展现出清晰的规模效应。
截⾄ 2026 年 9 ⽉ 30 ⽇，Anthropic 有约 250 家客⼾在过去 12 个⽉内分别贡献了⾄少 1000 万美元收

⼊；截⾄同期⼝径，公司拥有超过 1900 家过去 12 个⽉收⼊达到或超过 100 万美元的客⼾。这表
明其企业业务已形成⼀定规模，但公司仍需证明收⼊增⻓能够持续覆盖⽇益庞⼤的算⼒与研发
投⼊。
衡量客⼾留存与扩张能⼒的关键指标 ——净收⼊留存率约为 900%，意味着现有客⼾的平均消费
规模已扩⼤⾄原来的九倍。招股书显⽰，在今年第三季度， 79% 的营收来⾃前五⼤客⼾以外的
客⼾群体，客⼾结构的分散度进⼀步提升。
从收⼊结构看，基于消费量的收⼊ —— 即客⼾按实际使⽤的 Token 数量及模型类型付费 —— 增速
尤为迅猛。
该项⽬从 2024 年全年的 2.88 亿美元（占总营收 75% ）跃升⾄ 2025 年全年的 38 亿美元（占
83% ），并在 2026 年上半年进⼀步达到 131 亿美元，占当期总营收的 80% 。
公司上半年订阅收⼊ 32 亿美元，上年同期为 1.8 亿美元。
对于即将⾯对公开市场投资者的 Anthropic ⽽⾔，关键问题已不仅是收⼊能否继续⾼速增⻓，还
包括增⻓最终能否转化为可持续的经营利润和现⾦流。

算⼒承诺规模惊⼈：博通租赁⽀出约 1612 亿美元
招股书披露的基础设施承诺，是投资者评估 Anthropic 商业模式时绕不开的⼀环。前沿模型的训
练与推理需要持续投⼊计算资源，算⼒供给既决定产品能⼒，也直接影响成本结构。
根据申报⽂件， Anthropic 在与博通相关的租赁安排下，租赁期内租⾦⽀付总额约为 1612 亿美
元，且这⼀⾦额不包括利息。
此外，公司还披露了与其他算⼒及基础设施合作⽅的巨额⽀出安排：
SpaceX AI 及英伟达相关安排： Anthropic 同意在相关协议期限内⽀出总额最⾼约 1205 亿美
元，协议期限延续⾄ 2029 年。

### Page 3

Nscale 服务协议： 公司同意在相关协议期限内⽀出总额最⾼约 446 亿美元，协议期限延续⾄
2033 年。
这些数字代表协议约定期限内的⽀出承诺上限或总额，并不等于 Anthropic 已经⽀付了相同⾦
额，也不能直接视为单⼀年度的资本开⽀。
巨额合同凸显出 AI 企业⾯临的结构性挑战：为了维持模型性能、满⾜企业客⼾需求并争夺市场份
额，公司需要提前锁定⻓期算⼒供给；但随着合同规模扩⼤，投资者也将更关注未来收⼊能否
覆盖基础设施成本，以及算⼒需求和技术迭代发⽣变化时，公司是否仍能有效利⽤已承诺的资
源。
对于⼀家计划以超⼤规模估值上市的 AI 企业⽽⾔，这些⻓期⽀出安排将成为市场评估其增⻓质量
和财务⻛险的重要依据。
⽀付费半年增 2.22 亿美元逼近云软件开⽀，拟⾃建⾦融设施
Anthropic 规模化过程中的⼀项成本正快速膨胀：其⽀付处理费⽤上半年同⽐增加 2.22 亿美元，
增幅超过专业服务开⽀、逼近 IT 与云软件开⽀。
公司的应对⽅案，是评估⾃建计费、欺诈检测等⾦融基础设施 —— 这对其当前⽀付服务商 Stripe

的收⼊构成直接压⼒。
根据 S-1 ⽂件，Anthropic 上半年⽀付处理费⽤同⽐增加 2.22 亿美元，⾼于同期专业服务成本
2.05 亿美元的增幅，⼏乎追平 IT 及云软件成本 2.23 亿美元的增幅；同期与数据质量、性能评估相
关的供应商成本增加 2.51 亿美元。
公司近期招聘信息显⽰，其正在评估哪些⽀付相关服务可⾃⾏构建、⽽⾮继续依赖外部供应
商。
⽬前  Anthropic 的结账、欺诈检测、发票与计费均采⽤ Stripe 的产品。 Stripe 已崛起为 AI 公司的
主要⽀付供应商，这⼀业务推动其去年营收达到 68 亿美元。若 Anthropic 将部分⽀付功能内部
化， Stripe 的⼤客⼾收⼊稳定性将受到直接考验。
⻛险因素章节篇幅超 90 ⻚， Anthropic 提⽰⼈类 " ⽣存性⻛险 "
在招股书中， Anthropic ⻛险因素章节篇幅超 90 ⻚，其中就包括公司在 AI 安全与商业⽬标之间寻
求平衡所⾯临的⻛险。
招股书明确警⽰，公司 AI 系统可能以" ⾮预期、不对⻬或⾃主的⽅式运⾏ " ，若未能在充⾜的安全

保障与监管机制下推进技术开发，可能 " 对⼈类构成灾难性或存在性⻛险 "—— 尤其是在相关系统
遭到滥⽤、复制、武器化，或被允许以⾼度⾃主的⽅式运⾏的情形下。
招股书罗列的具体⻛险还包括 AI 系统 " 抵制关闭 " 及 " 隐瞒或操纵信息 " 等场景。
公司坦⾔： " 我们越来越多地使⽤⼈⼯智能系统来加速⾃⾝的研发，这可能会导致模型能⼒的提
升速度超过我们开发、验证和实施相应安全、对⻬及保障措施的能⼒。 "
⽂件显⽰，如果⽆法管控和 AI 安全相关的各类⻛险，公司的 " 品牌、业务、财务状况以及经营业
绩都可能遭受不利影响。 "Anthropic 称，公司会经常性作出主观判断，决定模型达到何种安全标
准才可以对外发布。

### Page 4

但同时也坦⾔，⽆法保证⾃⾝设置的安全防护措施⾜够有效，也⽆法保证公司能够准确识别哪
些模型能⼒应当加以限制。
招股书写道： " 这类判断⾄关重要。随着 AI 系统能⼒持续增强，⼀旦模型出现⽬标偏离或者被滥
⽤，其潜在危害程度也会同步上升。众多相关⽅，包括研究⼈员与政策制定者都提出担忧：如
果不能以负责任的⽅式研发、部署，⾜够先进的 AI 系统可能会给社会带来灾难性乃⾄⽣存级⻛
险。 "
这⼀表述的出现有其现实背景： Anthropic 与竞争对⼿ OpenAI 均⾯临⽇益加剧的公众审视。据
悉，⼀系列由失控代理主导的⾼知名度⽹络⼊侵事件接连发⽣，外界对此类事件冲击 Anthropic
IPO 的担忧正在升温。
从 " 存在性⻛险 " 到 " 可保性缺⼝ " ，⾼估值下的尾部⻛险定价
在⻛险因素部分，除了 AI 可能对⼈类构成灾难性或存在性⻛险外。市场还特别关注招股书中表
⽰：灾难性 AI 事件造成的损害，" 没有任何单⼀保险公司，甚⾄可能是整个保险⾏业合计 " 都⽆法
覆盖；公司坦⾔其保险 " 可能不⾜以 " 覆盖所⾯临的赔偿责任。

这则表述的分量在于其指向性。它没有停留在 " 部分损失可能超出单⼀保险公司承保能⼒ " 这类措
辞，⽽是直接把参照系拉到了 " 整个保险⾏业合计 " 的承保上限，实质上承认了⼀个结构性缺⼝。
传统估值框架中，极端尾部⻛险通常可通过保险转移或对冲来管理；当公司⾃⼰在招股书中承
认保险 " 可能不⾜以 " 覆盖最坏情形、且该情形可能超出整个保险业的承保能⼒时，投资者为这⼀
⻛险定价的锚点便被抽掉了。
对投资者⽽⾔，这意味着在评估这家⽬标估值可能⾼达 2 万亿美元的公司时，必须直⾯⼀个此前
未被充分定价的尾部⻛险 —— 在最坏情况发⽣时，作为传统⻛险缓释⼿段的保险机制可能整体
失效。
创始⼈通过 F 类股份掌握 50.1% 投票权
除了业务和财务信息， Anthropic 的股权结构也是此次 IPO ⽂件的⼀⼤看点。
申报⽂件显⽰，公司计划设置 A 类、 F 类、 E 类、 T 类和 N 类普通股，并通过特殊股权安排将创始
⼈的投票权与普通经济权益区分开来。
Anthropic 计划向由联合创始⼈组成的 Founder LLC 发⾏⼀股 F 类普通股。该实体的初始成员包括

⾸席执⾏官达⾥奥  · 阿莫迪 (Dario Amodei) 、丹妮拉 · 阿莫迪、汤姆 · 布朗、杰克 · 克拉克、贾⾥德
· 卡普兰、萨姆 · ⻨坎德利什和克⾥斯 · 奥拉。
这股 F 类普通股不享有经济权益，却拥有公司总投票权的 50.1% 。相⽐之下， A 类普通股每股拥
有⼀票表决权； N 类普通股不附带投票权。
其他类别股份也有各⾃的安排： E 类普通股仅对部分有限事项享有表决权，且不享有经济权益；
N 类普通股的持有⼈主要为战略业务合作伙伴，相关股份通常在出售或转让给⾮关联第三⽅时转
换为 A 类普通股。公司还表⽰， A 类普通股不可转换。
这套安排意味着， Anthropic 计划在引⼊公开市场投资者的同时，保留创始⼈对关键公司事务的
控制⼒。对投资者⽽⾔，特殊投票权结构有助于理解公司上市后的治理机制，也意味着普通股
股东的经济权益与实际投票影响⼒并不完全对等。

### Page 5

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

公共利益基⾦与捐赠匹配计划：治理承诺也进⼊招股书
Anthropic 还披露了与公共利益相关的安排。根据⽂件，公司董事会已批准⼀项框架：当股价每
上涨 50% 时，可向公共利益基⾦额外发⾏最⾼相当于 2% 的 N 类股份。
这项机制将股价表现与公共利益基⾦的股份配置联系起来，但具体实施仍应以招股书所披露的
完整条款及后续执⾏情况为准。
此外，截⾄ 2025 年 12 ⽉ 31 ⽇的财年， Anthropic 与捐赠匹配计划相关的费⽤为 5.41 亿美元。这⼀
数字反映了公司在相关计划上的财务⽀出，也表明公共利益安排已进⼊公司财务披露范围。
对于⼀家以 AI 安全与负责任开发为重要定位的公司⽽⾔，这些安排不仅涉及公司治理和资⾦使
⽤，也将成为公开市场投资者理解其⻓期战略的⼀部分。

IPO 考验：增⻓能否覆盖巨额投⼊？
Anthropic 此次公开申报，标志着其上市进程迈出关键⼀步。但从⾼达 2 万亿美元的市场估值预
期，到延伸⾄未来多年的算⼒合同，再到特殊投票权与公共利益基⾦安排，投资者需要评估的
远不只是 Claude 的市场热度。
⼀⽅⾯，超过 1900 家百万美元级客⼾显⽰出企业市场的商业化基础；另⼀⽅⾯，博通、 SpaceX
AI 、英伟达及 Nscale 等相关安排所涉及的巨额⽀出，也意味着公司未来仍需持续获得资⾦、算
⼒和稳定的企业需求。
与此同时， AI 相关股票近期承压，市场对资本开⽀回报的要求正在提⾼。即使 Anthropic 如期推
进 IPO ，最终发⾏估值和市场表现仍将取决于投资者如何权衡增⻓潜⼒、⻓期⽀出义务、盈利能
⼒及 AI ⾏业⻛险。

## Document 317: NZ CPI preview, September quarter 2026 (2026-10-26)

Source PDF: all report pdf/NZ CPI preview, September quarter 2026 (2026-10-26).pdf

### Page 1

Westpac New Zealand Economics
Stopping for gas
• We estimate that New Zealand consumer
prices rose by 0.7% in the September quarter,
with a moderation in fuel costs balanced
against increases in council rates, building
costs and food prices.
• The annual inflation rate is expected to drop
back to 3.7% (down from 4.1% in the year
to  June).
• Measures of core inflation are expected to
remain firm, with some closely watched
measures expected to rise to the top of the

RBNZ’s target band.
• Our forecast is a little below the RBNZ’s last
published forecast for 3.9% annual inflation.

RBNZ target range
We expect the September quarter inflation report (out
on 22 October) will show that New Zealand consumer
prices rose 0.7% over the past three months. That would
see the annual inflation rate slipping back to 3.7%, down
from 4.1% in the year to June. However, as we discuss

in our recent forecast update, that’s likely to be just a
temporary reprieve with the recent rise in oil prices set to
push inflation up again over the coming months.
16 Oct 2026 | Satish Ranchhod, Senior Economist | +64 9 336 5668 | +64 21 710 852 | satish.ranchhod@westpac.co.nz
ECONOMIC BULLETIN
CPI preview, September quarter 2026 –
Thursday 22 October, 10:45am.

### Page 2

2
Westpac New Zealand Economics
Key drivers of quarterly inflation.
Underpinning our forecast for 0.7% inflation in the
September quarter are some big price movements in
both directions.
The largest move in prices this quarter has actually been
a sharp fall in fuel costs. Fuel prices aren’t low by any
stretch of the imagination. However, average prices for
both petrol and diesel over the September quarter were
lower than in the June quarter when prices rocketed

higher in the wake of the Middle East conflict – see the
chart below. In terms of specifics, the average price for 91
unleaded was around 6% lower in September than in the
June quarter, while diesel prices were close to 16% lower.
Fuel spending accounts for around 4% of the CPI, and
these moves have taken 0.4ppts off inflation this quarter.
New Zealand petrol prices – 91 unleaded

While prices at the pump weren’t as high as they were in
the June quarter, fuel costs are still painfully high. And
that’s been bleeding through into higher prices for other
goods and services. Of note, the September quarter
saw large increases in passenger transport costs, with
international airfares up 14% and domestic airfares up

nearly 8%. Together those prices account for around 2%
of the CPI.
We also expect building costs (10% of the CPI) will rise by
1.6% this quarter following a similarly chunky rise in June.
Again, much of that rise has been related to higher global
oil prices which have been flowing through to higher
operating and material costs. That’s occurred at the same
time as the amount of planned construction work has
been rising.
The September quarter also sees the annual increases

in local council rates and charges for services like water
supply (around 4% of the CPI). We expect these costs will
be up around 7.4% this year, continuing the run of large
increases seen in previous years.
Lastly, the past few months saw a 1.0% rise in food prices
(18% of the CPI), largely as a result of the seasonal rise in
the price for fresh vegetables.
On the downside, we’ve seen continued softness in
housing rents, which are the largest component of

the CPI, accounting for 10% of the basket. With low
population growth and ample supply, rents have
effectively been flat since the start of this year. In annual
terms, rental inflation is the lowest it’s been in more
than two decades, with regions like Wellington seeing
outright falls.

Source: Westpac
Where could we be surprised?
There are some key areas where we see the risk
of a downside surprise. The first is the price for
communications equipment, like mobile phones. After
a large rise in the June quarter, we could see a sizeable

drop in prices this quarter.
There could also be softness in used car prices, especially
with high fuel prices encouraging a shift away from
combustion engine vehicles.
However, the risks aren’t all to one side. Demand for more
environmentally friendly new cars has picked up, and that
could push up prices for those vehicles. In addition, we’re
watching to see if high fuel costs are spilling over into
other prices as we’re seeing in sectors like construction.

Core inflation around 3%, not easing.
With big swings in fuel costs and concerns that this is
spilling over into a broader lift in inflation pressures,
the RBNZ will be paying close attention to the various
measures of core inflation (core inflation measures
smooth through quarter-to-quarter swings in individual
prices and instead track the underlying trend in inflation).
We expect that core inflation measures will point to
continued firmness in underlying price pressures, with

several closely watched measures likely to take a step
higher this quarter. In terms of specifics, we expect:
• CPI ex-fuel inflation: +3.0% yr (vs +2.9% previously)
• CPI ex-fuel and food: +3.2% yr (vs +2.9% previously)
• CPI ex-fuel, food and household energy: +2.9% yr (vs
+2.5% previously)

### Page 3

3
Westpac New Zealand Economics
We’ll also be watching the distribution of price
changes to see if there’s been a material increase in
the number of prices that are increasing. That could
be an early indication that businesses, even those
with a limited exposure to fuel costs, are starting to
factor in expectations of continued high inflation when
setting prices.
As we’ve noted before, the various measures of core
inflation were not low even before the recent fuel shock.

That means the RBNZ has limited headroom to absorb
upside inflation surprises.
Much of that firmness in core inflation has been due
to increases in domestically oriented non-tradables
prices. We estimate these prices rose by 1.3% over the
September quarter. That would see annual non-tradables
inflation rising to 3.6%, up from 3.4% last quarter.
Non-tradables inflation is a key focus for the RBNZ and
it’s been running at these above-average levels for around

a year now. That’s despite the softness in economic
growth and the labour market. In part, this firmness in
domestic inflation is due to continued large increases in
administered prices, like council rates.
On the imported front, lower fuel costs are expected to
see tradables prices falling by 0.2% over the quarter.
That would result in annual tradables inflation dropping
back to 3.9% from 4.9% previously. Excluding food and
fuel costs, we estimate that tradables prices were up

2.2% over the past year (vs 1.9% in the year to June). That
would be the highest level in three years with the fall in
the NZD boosting import costs.

Westpac
forecasts
How do our forecasts compare to the
RBNZ’s assumptions?
The RBNZ’s last published forecast from their September

policy statement assumed that consumer prices would
rise by 0.8%qtr (3.9%yr). That’s slightly higher than we’re
expecting. Most of the difference is because the RBNZ is
expecting higher imported inflation.
A result in line with our forecast would give the RBNZ a
little more breathing room ahead of their upcoming policy
review on 28 October. We’re expecting they will leave the
OCR on hold at this meeting, while markets are pricing in
a roughly 66% chance of a 25bp hike.

However, even if the overall inflation result is close to
forecast, the underlying detail will be important. The
RBNZ will be watching closely for signs that high fuel
prices are spilling over into other prices, especially with
global oil prices taking another step higher recently. With
that in mind, the strength of the various core inflation
measures will be a key focus for the RBNZ.
Furthermore, while annual inflation is expected to
soften in the September quarter, this is likely to be just a

temporary reprieve. With a sharp rise in fuel costs over
the past few weeks, inflation is set to reaccelerate back
over 4% before the end of this year, and it’s set to remain
above the RBNZ’s forecast for most of next year.
September monthly prices.
Our forecast for a 0.7% rise in consumer prices in the
September quarter incorporates information from
Stats NZ’s latest monthly prices update. That update
did reveal some softer than expected food price

movements in September. However, that was balanced
against larger than expected rises in airfares and holiday
accommodation costs.
See next page for charts of quarterly forecasts.

CENTRAL BANK MEETINGS

Monetary Authority of Singapore (MAS)

We expect another slight increase in the 
SGD NEER policy band slope at the MAS’ 
October review, with no change to the 
band’s width or centre. Import cost 
pressures and resilient growth favour a 
calibrated tightening, rather than a more 
forceful adjustment, in line with the 
global monetary policy stance.

Also due to be released alongside the 
MAS’ policy decision are the 3Q26 
advance GDP estimates, which we 
expect to show another quarter of strong 
growth at 5.4% yoy (1.4% qoq sa), 
compared with 5.9% yoy (1.4% qoq sa) in 
2Q26. Robust economic performance 
continued to be driven by artificial 
intelligence ( AI)-led trade activity, 
supporting the manufacturing and 
wholesale trade sectors, while strength 
in the financial sector underpinned the 
resilience of the modern services cluster. 
This came despite a moderation of the 
transport & storage sector, and 
weakness in consumer -facing food 
services and retail sectors.

FORTHCOMING DATA 
RELEASES

Malaysia

We expect Malaysia’s advance GDP 
estimate for 3Q26 to register above -
trend growth of 5.0% yoy, albeit 
moderating from 6.0% yoy  in 2Q26. The 
overall expansion continued to be 
supported by ongoing strength in the 
export-oriented manufacturing sector, 
underpinned by strong  AI-related 
demand for electronics, and sustained 
domestic demand, backed by healthy 
investment and private consumption 
trends. However, contraction in mining 
and weak agricultural activity dragged 
overall growth.

Singapore

We expect firm Singapore non -oil 
domestic exports (NODX) growth of 28.0% 
yoy in September 2026, although 
moderating from August’s exceptional 
surge of 46.2%. Although favourable 
base effects largely faded in September, 
electronics shipments likely remained 
the primary growth driver, underpinned 
by robust AI -related demand amid 
sustained global investment in AI 
infrastructure, while non-electronics 
exports were uneven.

### Page 3

The Week Ahead October 9, 2026 
                                                                                                                                         3

China

Exports growth is expected to remain 
robust at 25.7% y oy in September, 
supported by continued strength in 
electronics demand. Both the official and 
Caixin manufacturing PMIs rose above 
the 50 expansion threshold during the 
month, pointing to improving 
manufacturing activity. External trade 
indicators also stren gthened, with 
average daily deadweight tonnage 
handled at China's 20 major ports 
accelerating from -0.7% yoy in August to 
7.2% in September. Freight activity 
improved as well, as growth in 
international cargo flights picked up 
from -1.4% y oy to 3.6% over the same 
period.

On the price front, factory -gate and raw 
material prices rebounded amid the 
renewed escalation in geopolitical 
tensions. Main raw material purchasing 
price and producer price subPMI 
increased from 56.6 and 50.4 to 60.8 and 
54.0 during the period. However, 
downstream consumer price pressures 
are likely to remain relatively subdued 
under the pricing-band mechanism. As a 
result, CPI inflation is expected to edge 
up from 0.8% y oy in August to 1.0% in 
September. Nevertheless, soft domestic 
demand is expected to continue 
constraining the pace of consumer price 
increases.

India

September inflation is likely to gather 
momentum to 5.7% y/y, partly driven by 
base effects, from 4.8% the month 
before. Food segments continue to 
register gains, with pressures 
broadening to include perishables, 
edible oils, rice, pulses, sugar, etc., 
according to high-frequency data. Under 
non-food, retail pump prices have not 
been adjusted further, though other fuel 
products, such as non -subsidised LPG, 
diesel and ATF (still up by double digits 
y/y, though slower than in August), are 
up on the year. Core  inflation is also 
likely to tick up, aligning with the central 
bank's view that price risks are no longer 
benign. Pressures have built up due to 
the overhang of an uneven and sub -par 
monsoon, drought conditions in parts of 
the country, and elevated oil pr ices. In 
the face of higher headline prints, we 
expect the RBI to keep the door open for 
another rate hike in December 2026.

## Document 372: US Daily_ Why Has the US Economy Been So Resilient to Higher Rates and Higher Oil Prices_

Source PDF: all report pdf/US Daily_ Why Has the US Economy Been So Resilient to Higher Rates and Higher Oil Prices_.pdf
Report source: Goldman Sachs Publishing
Date: 2026-09-28
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Abhay Duggirala 
+1(202)585-6760 | 
abhay.duggirala@gs.com 
Goldman Sachs & Co. LLC
US Daily: Why Has the US Economy Been So Resilient to Higher Rates 
and Higher Oil Prices?
28 September 2026 | 10:38AM EDT
We estimate that the economy grew at a 3.4% pace in Q3 and forecast 2.2% n
growth in 2026 overall on a Q4/Q4 basis, close to our estimate of potential GDP 
growth and only 0.3pp below our start-of-the-year forecast before the large 
increases in interest rates and oil prices. Why, we are often asked, has the US 
economy proven so resilient to these shocks? 
While interest rates have risen substantially this year, our financial conditions n
index framework implies that the economic impact should be nearly offset by the 
effects of higher equity prices, narrower credit spreads, and a weaker dollar, 
resulting in only a 0.1pp net hit to 2026 GDP growth. While the net impact of this 
year’s market moves on overall GDP is modest, the effects on some parts of the 
economy—especially the hit to housing—are more meaningful. 
We estimate that the increase in oil and refined product prices this year, together n
with the path our commodities strategists expect through year-end, will subtract 
0.3pp from 2026 growth. The drag comes from consumption as higher energy 
prices erode real purchasing power. While US energy producers were reluctant to 
boost investment when oil prices first rose, the increase in the rig count index in 
recent months points to some offsetting boost to GDP in Q3 and Q4. 
Our analysis suggests that slightly tighter financial conditions and higher oil n
prices should together be roughly a 0.4pp drag on GDP growth. The downgrade 
we have made to our forecast for the year, in part based on incoming data, is only 
slightly smaller than that, with the slight outperformance coming largely from 
consumer spending. This suggests that the economy’s resilience is less puzzling 
than it might appear at first.

### Page 2

Why Has the US Economy Been So Resilient to Higher Rates and Higher Oil Prices?

We now estimate that the economy grew at a 3.4% pace in Q3 and forecast 2.2% growth 
in 2026 overall on a Q4/Q4 basis, close to our 2.3% estimate of potential GDP growth 
and only 0.3pp below our start-of-the-year forecast when we did not expect large 
increases in interest rates and oil prices. In today’s Daily, we ask why the US economy has 
proven so resilient to these surprises. 
Part of the answer is that financial conditions have changed little on net since January, as 
the substantial rise in interest rates has been nearly offset by the effects of higher equity 
prices, narrower credit spreads, and a weaker dollar (Exhibit 1, left). Our estimated GDP 
growth impulse from the FCI is therefore little changed from the start of the year (Exhibit 
1, right), implying only a 0.1pp hit to 2026 Q4/Q4 GDP growth.

While the net impact of this year’s market moves on overall GDP is modest, the effects 
on some components of GDP are more meaningful. We use the Fed’s macroeconomic 
model FRB/US to estimate how the particular combination of market moves this year 
should affect each component of GDP . We find a meaningful hit from higher rates to 
residential investment, offset by slight boosts to consumption and business investment 
(Exhibit 2).

### Page 3

What about the effect of higher oil and refined products prices? Our rule of thumb 
implies that a sustained $10/barrel increase in oil prices lowers GDP growth by just over 
0.1pp through the consumption channel as higher energy costs erode real purchasing 
power. Applied to the path oil prices have taken this year, the unusual widening in 
refined product spreads, and our commodities strategists’ forecasts for prices through 
year-end, the rule of thumb implies a 0.4pp drag on GDP growth. 
While US oil producers were initially reluctant to respond to higher prices by increasing 
capex because of the uncertainty of the war with Iran and the outlook for oil prices, the 
increase in the rig count index in Q3 (Exhibit 3) points to a pickup in energy capex more 
recently. We estimate that the consumption drag and capex boost will net out to a 0.3pp 
hit to GDP growth from the oil shock. Two alternative approaches, FRB/US simulations 
and a model estimated using exogenous oil shocks, deliver similar estimates on average.1 
1 We use the series of exogenous oil shocks from Känzig (2021), “The Macroeconomic Effects of Oil Supply 
News: Evidence from OPEC Announcements” to estimate the effects of the current oil shock on GDP growth.

### Page 4

Our analysis suggests that slightly tighter financial conditions and higher oil prices 
should together be roughly a 0.4pp drag on GDP growth. The downgrade we have made 
to our forecast for the year, in part based on incoming data, is only slightly smaller than 
that, suggesting that the puzzle of economic resilience is smaller than it might appear at 
first. 
The economy’s slight outperformance relative to what we might have expected largely 
comes from consumer spending. At the start of the year, we forecasted consumption 
growth of 2.4% in 2026 Q4/Q4. Under the market and commodity price moves that 
actually materialized, our oil shock model implies a 0.6pp hit to that forecast, partially 
offset by a 0.2pp boost from higher equity prices in our wealth effects model, netting to 
an expected 2.0% growth in consumption this year. Against that, we now estimate—in 
part based on the surprisingly solid incoming data in recent months—that consumption 
will grow 2.2%, a modest upside surprise of 0.2pp.

## Document 374: US Equity Views_ Over-earning, but not an earnings bubble

Source PDF: all report pdf/US Equity Views_ Over-earning, but not an earnings bubble.pdf

### Page 2

semiconductor share prices have recently lagged the trajectory of near-term EPS
estimates. Similarly, the market trades at a P/E discount relative to what a macro model
would imply given the current level of corporate profitability. Our 12-month S&P 500
return forecast of +14% (to 8,700) reflects the view that earnings growth, rather than
expanding valuations, will remain the primary driver of the bull market.
17 September 2026   2
Goldman Sachs US Equity Views

### Page 3

The magnitude of S&P 500 “over-earning”

S&P 500 earnings grew by an extraordinary 51% year/year in Q2 2026 and have 
grown by 26% during the past four quarters. During the last 30 years, the recent pace 
of earnings growth has been exceeded only during the post-recession rebounds in 2010 
and 2021.

The recent strength of S&P 500 earnings growth has raised investor concerns that 
current earnings power is unsustainable and that the market is in an “earnings 
bubble.” S&P 500 earnings have recently outpaced both their historical trend and their 
historical relationship with US GDP growth. S&P 500 4-quarter EPS growth has averaged 
7% during the past 30 years, and the relationship between S&P 500 earnings and US 
economic growth in recent decades would have pointed to a similar rate of earnings 
growth during the last few quarters.

### Page 4

The degree to which the US equity market is currently “expensive” depends in large 
part on the sustainability of recent earnings strength. The exceptional recent 
strength of earnings has driven an unusual wedge between equity valuation multiples 
based on near-term earnings and valuations on trend earnings. Because equity prices 
have failed to keep pace with surging earnings, near-term valuations show no hint of a 
bubble: The S&P 500 12-month P/E ratio has declined from 23x a year ago to 19x today 
and now matches its 10-year average. However, even an “average” multiple may be 
expensive if current earnings are unsustainable. The cyclically-adjusted P/E based on 
trailing 10-year earnings has climbed to one of its highest levels on record, falling shy of 
the highs of 1999-2000 but surpassing the level reached in 2021. Similarly, our 
colleagues have argued that the increase in market value of AI companies during the last 
few years requires an optimistic estimate of the future magnitude of AI-related profits 
and who will capture them.

### Page 5

The AI boom and S&P 500 earnings growth

Affirming investor concerns that US companies are “over-earning,” there are three 
factors that are boosting earnings today but will contribute to deceleration going 
forward: 1) AI capex spending, 2) semiconductor margin expansion, and 3) earnings 
from private investment gains.

AI capex 
The AI investment boom is driving nearly half of S&P 500 EPS growth this year, but 
its contribution will fade going forward. The mega-cap US hyperscalers are on track to 
spend $800 billion on capex this year, an increase of 94% vs. 2025. That spending is 
flowing through to the earnings of the AI infrastructure complex including 
semiconductor, tech hardware, industrials, and utilities companies, which are collectively 
accounting for roughly half of consensus S&P 500 earnings growth this year. The AI 
boom is also having a secondary impact on earnings outside of the infrastructure 
complex, for example by boosting capital markets activity and consumer wealth. Both 
consensus and GS analyst forecasts show hyperscaler capex growing at a slower rate in 
coming years, which should result in decelerating earnings growth for much of the AI 
infrastructure complex and a fading tailwind to S&P 500 earnings.

Hyperscaler depreciation expenses will continue to increase as capex growth 
decelerates, further dampening the boost of AI investment spending to S&P 500 
earnings growth. We estimate a drag from hyperscaler depreciation expenses on S&P 
500 earnings growth of 5pp in 2027, offsetting nearly half of the 11pp boost to earnings 
from capex spending. By 2028, the drag from depreciation should offset the S&P 500 
earnings uplift from continued capex spending.

### Page 7

If capex were fully expensed, consensus 2027 S&P 500 EPS would be 22% lower than 
under current accounting rules. The current gap between actual reported and 
hypothetical fully-expensed earnings is one of the widest in recent decades, exceeded 
only as earnings fell more quickly than capex during the recessions of 2001 and 2008.

Hyperscaler capex has consistently surprised relative to consensus estimates during 
the last few years, and the potential for additional surprises going forward creates a 
wide range of potential S&P 500 earnings outcomes. Our equity analysts expect 
hyperscaler capex will total $1.2 trillion in 2027 and $1.4 trillion in 2028, in which case 
the current tailwind from AI capex to S&P 500 EPS will fade by 2028. Exhibit 10 below 
outlines upside and downside scenarios around that base case. Next year, a $250 billion 
surprise in hyperscaler capex — in either direction — would shift S&P 500 EPS growth by 
about 6 pp in the same direction.  
We estimate that hyperscaler capex would need to decline by roughly 30%, to $570 
billion, to offset earnings growth for the rest of the S&P 500 in 2027. This decline 
would reduce earnings for the AI infrastructure complex by about 40%, leaving their 
profits about 25% above 2025 levels.

### Page 8

Semiconductor margin expansion 
A sharp increase in profit margins for many semiconductor companies has 
contributed to the above-trend recent pace of S&P 500 earnings growth. The 
combination of strong demand and limited supply has boosted semiconductor prices 
and gross margins, with the most pronounced margin expansion among memory stocks. 
S&P 500 memory firms currently enjoy gross margins of roughly 80%, more than double 
their historical average. Across the semiconductor industry, gross margins sit at their 
highest level in decades, and consensus expects margins to remain at similar levels 
through 2028.

Margin expansion has accounted for a large share of recent semiconductor earnings 
growth, but that boost should fade going forward. We estimate that about 1/4 of S&P 
500 Semiconductor earnings growth in 2026 is being driven by gross margin expansion. 
Our equity analysts believe the balance of memory supply and demand is likely to 
remain tight through 2027, with incremental supply additions unlikely to impact industry 
margins until 2028 at the earliest. However, they expect the rate of incremental margin 
expansion to slow meaningfully next year, reducing the contribution of semiconductors

S&P 500 earnings growth in the next few years will be highly sensitive to the 
trajectory of semiconductor margins. In a scenario where slowing AI infrastructure 
investment, increasing supply, and/or technological shift lowers semiconductor prices 
and profit margins, S&P 500 EPS growth would also disappoint. We estimate that each 
percentage point change in S&P 500 semiconductor gross margins next year would shift 
S&P 500 EPS growth by about 1 pp.

### Page 10

“Other income” from private investment gains 
Appreciating equity investment stakes are also temporarily inflating S&P 500 
earnings. Mega-cap tech earnings have recently been boosted by large GAAP “other 
income” related to their equity investments. In Q2 2026, the mega-cap tech companies 
enjoyed unrealized investment gains in private companies that totaled roughly $150 
billion, translating into 12% of S&P 500 EPS. We expect H2 2026 reports will include 
additional “other income” that should be larger than the gains in Q1 but smaller than Q2 
totals. We expect a much smaller contribution in 2027. The complete removal of this 
“other income” next year would create a drag of 8 pp on S&P 500 earnings growth in 
2027 relative to 2026, all else equal.

### Page 11

S&P 500 earnings outlook

Our baseline forecast is for S&P 500 EPS to rise to $415 in 2027 (+11% year/year) 
and to $460 in 2028 (+11%). We expect an above-consensus base of earnings in 2026 
due to further “other income” gains from private equity stakes in H2 of this year. 
Excluding the “other income” inflating earnings in 2026, S&P 500 earnings growth next 
year would register 18%. Our 2027 EPS forecast is similar to the bottom-up consensus 
estimate of $419 but above the median top-down strategist estimate of $403.

### Page 12

Our 2028 EPS forecast is about 5% below the bottom-up consensus, signaling a 
return to the typical historical pattern of downward revisions to consensus earnings 
estimates after the last two years of rising estimates. Outside of the complex of AI 
capex beneficiaries, we expect sales growth will register close to nominal GDP , margins 
will expand modestly, and a growing boost from AI productivity will result in earnings 
growth of 9% in both 2027 and 2028.  Our forecasts for EPS growth among this group of 
companies are roughly 4-5% below consensus estimates in both 2027 and 2028, similar 
to the historical average magnitude of downward revision to consensus estimates. While 
AI investment spending has had a direct impact on the earnings of semiconductor and 
other AI infrastructure stocks, it has also lifted earnings for companies outside of the “ AI 
complex,” including by boosting capital markets activity as well as supporting consumer 
spending via the wealth effect. 
Solid GDP growth and easing pressure from energy prices will be the main macro 
tailwinds for earnings growth in 2027. Economic growth is the primary variable in our 
top-down earnings model and explains more than half of the historical variation in S&P 
500 EPS growth. Our economists expect US real GDP growth will average 2.1% in 2027 
and 2.3% in 2028. Our commodity strategists expect Brent crude oil will decline to 
$80/bbl by the end of 2027, which would weigh modestly on Energy sector profits but 
boost earnings for most other sectors. Outside of the AI-related risks outlined above, 
persistent input cost pressures and risks to the consumer from equity market volatility 
are two of the key macro downside risks to our earnings forecast.

### Page 13

As the earnings tailwind from AI investment spending fades, the trajectory of S&P 
500 profitability in coming years will depend increasingly on the realization of AI 
productivity gains. Our forecasts embed a 1 pp boost to S&P 500 EPS growth from AI 
productivity in 2027 and a 2 pp boost in 2028. Economy-wide surveys and corporate 
commentary signal enterprise adoption that is increasing but remains in early stages, 
and the impact of AI adoption on corporate earnings still appears narrow. However, the 
recent acceleration in enterprise AI spending suggests that the earnings impact of AI 
adoption should become clearer in coming quarters.

### Page 14

Valuing S&P 500 earnings

Like our forecasts and consensus estimates, market pricing suggests investors 
anticipate a deceleration in earnings growth going forward. This is true both for the 
broad market and for AI infrastructure stocks. For example, the share prices of 
semiconductor stocks have historically mirrored the path of consensus EPS estimates, 
and the recent moderation of semiconductor returns has aligned with consensus 
estimates for decelerating growth in 2027 and 2028.

The current S&P 500 P/E multiple signals an optimistic outlook but market 
skepticism regarding the sustainability of current profits. In addition to rising interest 
rates, an anticipated deceleration in earnings helps explain the recent decline in the S&P 
500 forward P/E multiple. Along with macro factors like interest rates, the level of 
corporate profitability has helped explain the variation in S&P 500 valuation over time. 
Based on the historical relationship between S&P 500 ROE and P/E, the current S&P 500 
ROE of 24% — a record high — would point to a forward 12-month P/E of over 21x 
against today’s macro backdrop. Given current inflation and interest rates, today’s S&P 
500 multiple of 19x would be consistent with an ROE of roughly 22%, more than 200 bp 
below the current level but otherwise matching 2021 as the highest on record.

Earnings, rather than valuations, should continue to drive equity market upside 
from here. Our 3-, 6-, and 12-month S&P 500 return targets are +5 (8000), +9% (8300),

close to the current level of 19x. Our economists and interest rate strategists expect 
interest rates to decline modestly in coming quarters, creating some macro upside risk to 
equity valuations. However, we expect investor uncertainty regarding the impact of AI 
on the outlook for long-term profits to persist in coming quarters, making a dramatic 
increase in valuations unlikely.

## Document 376: US SECTORS CALL_ Communication Services, Materials & Energy

Source PDF: all report pdf/US SECTORS CALL_ Communication Services, Materials & Energy.pdf
Report source: researchmail轉寄
Date: 2026-10-26
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: US SECTORS CALL: Communication Services, Materials & Energy
Date:    7 hours ago
US SECTORS CALL: Communication Services, Materials & Energy

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
US SECTORS CALL:
Communication Services,
Materials & Energy
By Ed Yardeni & Toby Hearst • 25 Oct 2026
View in browser
Photo by Noah Klassen / Unsplash

### Page 2

Investors spent the week worrying about an AI bubble. The S&P 500
slipped by 0.5%, led by a 6.2% drop in Communication Services (MW),
which was the worst-performing S&P 500 sector (table). Yet, Information
Technology fell just 0.3%. Energy (OW) led the winning sectors with a
1.6% gain, followed by Health Care (OW), up 1.5%.
Bubbles inflate valuation multiples. This year, multiples have contracted.
The S&P 500's forward P/E has fallen to 18.6 from 22.2 at the end of 2025

(chart). Information Technology's forward P/E is down to 19.0 from 26.5.
Stocks have gotten cheaper this year as earnings growth outpaced stock
price gains.
Here's more on the Communication Services, Materials, and Energy
sectors:

### Page 3

(1) Communication Services. Meta's Q3 report last Wednesday hit the
sector hard. Its free cash flow turned negative for the first time since its
2012 IPO, and it raised its 2026 capex guidance floor to $135 billion. Its
shares fell 7.0% and Interactive Media & Services fell 4.3% on Thursday,
closing down 7.0% for the week. Alphabet and Meta dominate the sector.
Together they account for 81.8% of its market cap and 70.0% of its
forward earnings (chart).

The sector's forward earnings is up 23.9% ytd, while its stock price index
is down 2.3% (chart). Its forward P/E has dropped to 17.2 from 22.1 at the
start of the year. That puts it below the S&P 500's 18.6.
Analysts expect earnings at Interactive Media & Services, the sector's
largest industry, to rise 72.3% this year, then fall 17.6% in 2027 (chart).
Yet they expect its revenues to grow 25.0% and 22.8% over the same two

### Page 4

years. Unrealized gains on Alphabet's stakes in private AI companies,
including Anthropic, inflated this year's earnings.
The de-rating isn't limited to AI. Netflix, which accounts for more than half
of Movies & Entertainment's market cap, reported Q3 results on Tuesday.
It cut its ad revenue outlook and guided Q4 earnings below expectations.
Its stock is now down 51% from its mid-2025 record high.
(2) Materials. Materials slipped 0.1% this week. Gold and Copper were

the sector's worst-performing industries, down 8.2% and 6.3% (chart).
Copper stocks fell along with the AI trade on Thursday.
Copper futures also fell slightly last week, ending at $6.48 per pound. The
AI selloff hit industrial metals on Thursday, but copper rebounded on
Friday. Rising inventories also weighed on the red metal last week.

### Page 5

Copper flowed back into London Metal Exchange warehouses, where
inventories rose to their highest since July. Traders spent the year
shipping copper to the US ahead of potential tariffs. The US price
premium that drove those flows has turned into a discount. Copper
remains near the top of an uptrend channel that began in 2016 (chart).
Forecasts have copper earnings rising 76.3% this year and 41.2% in
2027. The forward profit margin is up to 17.1%, the highest since 2022,

yet the forward P/E has dropped to 16.4. The selloff hit stock prices, not
earnings estimates.
Analysts expect Materials earnings to rise 40.2% this year and 11.8% in
2027. Materials accounts for just 1.9% of S&P 500 forward earnings and
only 1.6% of its market cap, the smallest share in over 30 years (chart).

### Page 6

We remain overweight. Materials offers rising earnings at a shrinking
multiple.
(3) Energy. Energy is up 55.1% ytd, the best-performing sector. Its
forward earnings has risen even faster, up 76.5% ytd to a record high
(chart). Energy still has the lowest forward P/E of the 11 sectors, at 13.8,
down from 15.7 at the start of the year.
Analysts see 2026 as the peak. They expect sector earnings to rise
101.9% this year, then fall 8.0% in 2027. Refining & Marketing shows this

most clearly. Its forward profit margin has soared to a record 9.0% (chart).
Its forward P/E is 9.0, with earnings forecast to fall 11.0% in 2027.

### Page 7

Oil & Gas Equipment & Services is the exception. Analysts expect its
earnings to fall 9.7% this year, then rise 25.2% in 2027 and 18.9% in 2028
(chart).
Consensus estimates assume the oil premium fades, and we expect it will
eventually. Renewed strikes on Iran after the midterms, which the US and
Israel reportedly have planned, would challenge the fading-premium
assumption. At only 3.7% of the S&P 500's market cap and a forward P/E
of 13.8, Energy is a cheap hedge against more geopolitical turmoil.

長榮航太 2645 TT  ( 航運業 ) 
長空展翼，航向新高 
投資與評價 
1. 長榮航太 3Q26 受惠維修需求穩健，營收維持季增，惟製造待料、原物料
成本偏高及管理費用增加壓抑獲利，預估 EPS 1.90 元；4Q26 維修需求與
製造出貨動能延續，惟年終獎金及相關人事費用增加，EPS 預估 1.77 元。 
2. 26 年受惠維修工時費率調升、既有機隊延長使用，獲利預估創歷史新高；
27 年維修業務透過人力補充與周轉率改善 ， 製造業務則受惠發動機零組件
需求及空巴 A321 新案投產 ， 帶動營運續創新高 ， 預估26 / 27 年 EPS 8.30 
/ 9.40 元。 
3. 目前長榮航太股價 170.5 元，約為 18.1 倍 FY27(F) PER，位於近三年 15-
35 倍本益比區間中間偏下位置。考量維修業務需求穩定，製造產能維持滿
載。製造業務受惠波音 737 MAX 10 重回民航市場，惟無人機業務仍需等
待，維持買進投資評等，因調整 26、27 年獲利預估，目標價調高至 210
元(22x 27(F)PER)。 
重點更新 
長榮航太 3Q26 維修需求支撐，成本增加但營收維持成長 
3Q26 維修業務受惠棚廠持續接近滿載 ， 新機交付未能完全滿足航空公司需求 ，
帶動既有機隊延長使用年限，支撐定期檢修及結構修復業務。製造業務則受惠
發動機零組件需求增加，但原料到貨時間不穩定，影響生產排程與出貨效率，
營收增加尚未充分轉化為獲利改善。 3Q26 營收 52.75 億元(+1.0% QoQ 、
+17.5% YoY)，優於先前預估 3.9%。惟製造業務原物料成本仍高，下調毛利率
預估至 21.4%(+0.1ppts QoQ) ；因上調管理費用預估，營業費用上調至 2.45
億元(+10.1% QoQ)，營業利益 8.82 億元(-0.9% QoQ)。預估稅後淨利 7.11 億
元(-5.6% QoQ)，EPS 1.90 元，營收較去年同期維持成長。 
長榮航太 4Q26 營運受人事成本壓抑，本業營運創歷史同期新高 
4Q26 維修業務受惠既有機隊檢修需求及工時費率調整，棚廠維持滿載。製造
業務隨客戶生產需求增加，維持出貨動能另外，第四季通常認列年終獎金及相
關人事成本，毛利率與營業費用率相對 3Q26 承壓。考量維修人事相關成本提
升，下調4Q26 營收預估 3.4%至 52.26 億元(-0.9% QoQ)，同步下調毛利率至
20.4%(-1.0ppts QoQ)；因預期員工福利費用與研發費用增加，上調營業費用
預估至 2.62 億元(+6.7% QoQ)，但營業利益仍年增 67.2%至 8.05 億元(-8.8% 
QoQ)。預估稅後淨利 6.64 億元(-6.7% QoQ、+39.2% YoY)，EPS 1.77 元，
本業營運創歷史同期新高。

長榮航太 3Q26 維修需求支撐，成本增加但營收維持成長 
長榮航太 3Q26 維修業務受惠棚廠持續接近滿載，新機交付未能完全滿足航空公司需求，帶動既
有機隊延長使用年限，支撐定期檢修及結構修復業務。製造業務則受惠發動機零組件需求增加，
但原料到貨時間不穩定，影響生產排程與出貨效率，營收增加尚未充分轉化為獲利改善。 
3Q26 營收 52.75 億元(+1.0% QoQ、+17.5% YoY)，優於先前預估 3.9%。惟製造業務原物料成
本仍高， 下調毛利率預估至21.4%(+0.1ppts QoQ)；因上調管理費用預估 ，營業費用上調至 2.45
億元(+10.1% QoQ)，營業利益 8.82 億元(-0.9% QoQ)。預估稅後淨利 7.11 億元(-5.6% QoQ)，
EPS 1.90 元，營收較去年同期維持成長。

長榮航太 4Q26 營運受人事成本壓抑，本業營運創歷史同期新高 
長榮航太 4Q26 維修業務受惠既有機隊檢修需求及工時費率調整，棚廠維持滿載。製造業務則
隨客戶新機與發動機生產需求增加，維持出貨動能，惟原料到貨時間不穩定，仍影響生產效率與
營收。另外，第四季通常認列年終獎金及相關人事成本，毛利率與營業費用率相對 3Q26 承壓。 
考量維修人事相關成本提升，下調 4Q26 營收預估 3.4%至 52.26 億元(-0.9% QoQ)，同步下調
毛利率至 20.4%(-1.0ppts QoQ)；因預期員工福利費用與研發費用增加，上調營業費用預估至
2.62 億元(+6.7% QoQ)，但營業利益仍年增 67.2%至 8.05 億元(-8.8% QoQ)。預估稅後淨利
6.64 億元(-6.7% QoQ、+39.2% YoY)，EPS 1.77 元，本業營運創歷史同期新高。

維修調價與業外挹注，長榮航太 26 年營運將創高 
展望 26 年，長榮航太製造業務雖隨客戶新機與發動機交付需求增加而成長，但所用原物料受烏俄
戰爭影響，產線效率及改善速度仍在緩步改善。維修業務則受惠先前逐客戶協商調整工時費率，
價格效益持續反映，航空公司延長舊機使用亦支撐維修工作量。現有棚廠接近滿載， 公司將持續
投入技術人員培訓，以利未來增加承接量推升營運。 
長榮航太 26 年營收年增 12.6%至 204.61 億元，受人事與製造成本增加，略下調毛利率預估至
22.5%；營業費用 9.06 億元(+30.3% YoY)，營業利益為 37.06 億元(+23.5% YoY)。稅後淨利預估
3.2%至 31.07 億元(+52.0% YoY)，EPS 8.30 元，營運表現創下歷史新高。

737 MAX 過認證、空巴新案帶動，長榮航太 27 年營運續寫歷史新高 
波音 737 MAX 10 近期已取得 FAA 認證，解除該機型交付的重要限制。該機型訂單逾1,550 架，
約占波音 737 系列待交訂單 31.0%，有助提升後續生產與交付能見度。長榮航太承作其中 LEAP
發動機燃燒段相關產品，應用涵蓋 737 MAX 及空巴 A320neo 系列，研究部認為，後續原廠若順
利提高產量，將有助增加零組件拉貨並改善製造產能利用率。維修業務方面，27 年營運重點轉向
人力補充、第二班安排及維修周轉率改善。現有四座棚廠接近滿載，第五座棚廠須等待桃園機場
土地分配，因此27 年營運仍以既有棚廠效率提升為主。製造業務則除既有發動機零組件外，觀音
二廠預計 2Q27 完工、3Q27 開始投單生產，承接空巴A321 機身件。長異發動機則已恢復現場作
業，將緩步重回過往水準。 
故預估 27 年營收 220.01 億元(+7.5% YoY)。 受惠維修需求穩健 、 製造出貨增加及營運效率改善 ，
預估毛利率 23.4%； 營業費用8.06 億元(-11.0% YoY)， 預估營業利益為43.48 億元(+17.3% YoY)。
稅後淨利 35.20 億元(+13.7% YoY)，EPS 9.40 元，營運續寫歷史新高。

個股報告 
2026/10/22

波音 737 製造回溫、無人機業務添翼，維持長榮航太買進評等 
目前長榮航太股價 170.5 元，約為18.1 倍 FY27(F) PER，位於近三年 15-35 倍本益比區間中間偏下
位置。考量波音 737 MAX 10 近期取得 FAA 認證，該機型佔波音 737 系列待交訂單約 31%，逾
1550 架，認證後也降低後續交付不確定性，長榮航太 LEAP 發動機燃燒段相關產品出貨有望增加，
加上空巴新廠亦提供長榮航太中期成長機會。考量 26 年維修業務穩健成長，加上費率調整與業外
改善支撐獲利，預估 EPS 8.30 元；27 年則由既有維修需求、製造出貨增加及費用回落帶動成長，
預估 EPS 9.40 元，營運有望持續走高。 
無人機業務方面，先前 9 月行政院通過《強化國防自主暨無人載具產業發展條例》 ，連續六年編列 
2,400 億元，每年以 400 億元為原則。政策範圍涵蓋可信任供應鏈、研發等。此外，行政院也提出
的 1,457 億元國防追加預算，其中「濱海監偵型」 、 「濱海攻擊型無人機」及「小型自殺無人艇」三
項合計 559 億元 。 長榮航太製造量能充足 ， 加上具備既有監偵型無人載具交付經驗 ， 後續將持續爭
取新案。目前國防部已完成招商說明，希望在預算通過後加快採購，於 2027 年形成初始戰力，目
前仍待國防部公布相關規格。 
考量長榮航太維修業務需求穩定，以及既有製造產能維持滿載。27 年製造業務受惠波音 737 MAX 
10 重回民航市場，無人機業務提供潛在成長機會，維持買進投資評等。惟當前國防部仍未公布無人
機採購規格，仍要等待更多採購細節釋出，本益比由接近平均值之 24 倍下調至中間偏下之 22 倍，
並以 27 年獲利預估，目標價由 195 元(24x 26(F)PER)調高至 210 元(22x 27(F)PER)。

公司簡介 
長榮航太，(Evergreen Aviation Technologies Corporation，簡稱 EGAT）於 1997 年成立，總部
位於桃園市大園區，為長榮集團旗下、長榮航空持股55.05%的航空維修與航太製造公司。主要業務
分為航空器維修及航空零組件製造兩大領域，服務涵蓋機體定期維修、飛機改裝、零組件修護，以
及機體與發動機零組件製造、加工及組裝。2Q26 營收為 52.22 億元，其中維修業務 40.90 億元
(78%)；製造業務 11.32 億元(22%)。 
維修業務方面 ， 公司取得美國聯邦航空總署 （FAA） 、 歐盟航空安全署（EASA） 及日本民航局 （JCAB）
等主管機關的維修認證，除支援長榮航空與立榮航空機隊外，亦承接國際航空公司維修需求。既有
維修機型涵蓋波音 747、767、777 及 787 等，並持續拓展新世代機型服務。依 2026 年 5 月法說
資料，公司已於 1Q26 取得 EASA 的 A350 維修認證，為拓展外部客戶及支援長榮航空預計 2027
年導入 A350 機隊預作準備。 
製造業務方面，公司既有客戶包括波音與GE Aerospace，並於 2025 年與空中巴士簽署合作合約，
成為其一階機體組件供應商，擴大國際民航製造供應鏈布局。此外，公司亦拓展電子產業設備製造
及無人機業務，增加航空維修以外的成長來源。後續營運重點包括新機型維修客戶開拓、空巴新案
導入，以及新廠產能建置與量產進度。 
轉投資方面，長榮航太持有長異發動機維修 49%、長銳航材 49%及長鑽科技 9.3%股權，均於財報
列為採權益法認列的關聯企業。長異為與 GE 合作的發動機及零件維修事業；長銳主要從事航空器
零組件維修；長鑽則提供金屬製品表面化學處理服務，補強公司在發動機、零組件修護及製造製程
的布局。
